### [1] [Real-World Adoption, Use, and Governance of an LLM-Based Electronic Health Record Search and Synthesis Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08672)
*Jiunn-Tyng Yeh,Michael Gao,Marshall Nichols,Anika Mohan,Tareq Aljurf,Michael Revoir,Matthew Gardner,Bradley Hintze,William Ratliff,Suresh Balu,Will Knechtle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 报告Scout（集成电子健康记录、提供带引用检索与综合的大语言模型平台）在多院区学术医疗系统分阶段部署11个月的纵向采用情况：2,635名用户完成80,465项任务，上线首日启动率提升21.2倍，采用覆盖20余个专科、5类临床角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估临床场景中大语言模型工具的真实世界纵向采用与使用模式，为规模化部署提供治理、培训与容量规划依据。

Method: 多医院学术健康系统分阶段部署的纵向观察性分析；追踪用户启动曲线、按需培训（必修课程）完成与后续使用的关系，并进行探索性提示词分析（检索、综合、提取、评价、草稿撰写），辅以用量集中度（五分位）与token统计。

Result: 11个月内2,635名用户完成80,465项任务；1,768名临床用户在上线后29天内启动使用；日启动用户从2.9增至61.0（21.2倍）；上线周495名启动者中310名集中于前两日。使用覆盖20余专科、5类照护角色、46,485条不同提示、752项任务被保存复用。完成必修课程者中77.9%随后使用，课程完成后中位1.7天启用。最高强度五分位用户贡献59.0%任务量与61.9%token。

Conclusion: 快速广泛采用依赖于：临床医生主导的任务设计与评价、与覆盖范围相匹配的可追溯治理、集中式容量规划与用户支持，以及访问权限与培训的一体化整合。

Abstract: We evaluated the longitudinal adoption and use of Scout, an electronic health record-integrated large language model platform that provides cited search and synthesis of patient records, during staged deployment across a multi-hospital academic health system. Over 11 months, 2,635 users completed 80,465 tasks. 1,768 clinical users initiated use within the first 29 days of system-wide launch, which included a required training. Daily initiation increased 21.2-fold at launch, from 2.9 to 61.0 users per day. Of the 495 users who initiated use during the launch week, 310 did so in the first two days. Use spanned more than 20 recorded specialty categories, five care-practitioner roles, and 46,485 distinct prompts involving retrieval, synthesis, extraction, evaluation, and drafting, with 752 tasks saved for reuse. Among care practitioners who completed the required course, 77.9% went on to use Scout, with a median of 1.7 days after course completion. The highest-intensity user quintile accounted for 59.0% of tasks and 61.9% of tokens. Exploratory prompt analysis showed use across retrieval, synthesis, extraction, evaluation, and note-drafting. These findings suggest that rapid, broad adoption requires clinician-led task design and evaluation, traceable governance proportional to reach, centralized capacity planning and user support, and training integrated with access.

</details>


### [2] [Remembering Is Not Enough: Evaluating Timing and Omission in Longitudinal AI Mental Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08721)
*Eli Min,Mohit Chandra,Aamogh Sawant,Mengting Tu,Freya Lin,Ridwan Shams Khan,Reigna El-Yashruti,Christopher L. Smith,Mark Kalinich,John Torous,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Evaluations of large language models (LLMs) for mental health support commonly focus on the clinical appropriateness of individual responses, potentially overlooking failures that emerge across extended interactions. As AI assistants accumulate memory of users' circumstances and prior disclosures, this raises an accountability question: Do AI chatbots provide appropriate support as users' needs evolve? We investigate by analyzing 14,256 longitudinal simulations grounded in donated chatbot histories from 22 users, examining how a memory-equipped assistant responds to suicidal ideation, self-harm, and anxiety crises across recovery trajectories and communication styles. We introduce the Context-Aware and Readiness-Evaluative (CARE) framework, developed with mental health professionals, to assess the timing and quality of advice, including omissions. Advice was less aligned with users' readiness after a crisis than in matched control stretches of the same conversation, largely because of premature recommendations, which were most common when recovery stalled. Revisiting a crisis reduced premature advice but not lagging advice, and users' communication style changed how often and when the assistant gave recommendations. Although explicitly unproductive advice was rare, the assistant frequently omitted clinician-expected support such as follow-up and planning for next steps. These findings show the limits of single response-level safety evaluation and the need to assess timing, omission, and consistency across users when holding AI systems accountable for longitudinal support.

</details>


### [3] [How are viral trends different from educational and commercial uses of LLM image generation and editing? A privacy-preserving descriptive analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08749)
*Will Tucker-Ray,Marika Swanberg,Alexander Knop,Marvin Chow,Eric Culbertson,Julian Jacobs,Pritish Kamath,Ravi Kumar,Jason Mothersill,Mikhail Pravilov,Eugene Quan,Yurii Sushko*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文利用Google的差分隐私框架Urania，在不查看任何单张图像或单条提示的前提下，对2026年初使用Nano Banana图像生成/编辑模型的210万段对话进行聚类分析，归纳出五大使用类别并在21个特征上进行比较，给出隐私保护下的图像生成与编辑使用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nano Banana模型（2025年末推出）显著改变了图像生成与编辑的能力与使用方式，但研究真实用户如何使用这类工具通常需要接触敏感的图像与提示数据，存在隐私风险。作者希望在严格差分隐私保障下刻画用户的使用场景与意图，建立可参考的使用基线。

Method: 使用Google开发的Urania框架（具备严格差分隐私保证、可对LLM聊天机器人交互生成洞察）；抽样2026年初涉及Nano Banana模型的210万段对话，通过聚类得到约800个簇，归入五类：(1)社交与病毒式传播、(2)品牌与营销物料、(3)教育与技术信息媒介、(4)产品可视化与电商、(5)建筑与空间设计；随后在提示词的(a)编辑技巧、(b)艺术风格请求、(c)构图、(d)聊天交互四方面共21个特征上比较各簇。全程不查看单张图像或单条提示。

Result: 得到覆盖五大使用类别的约800个簇，并在21个使用特征上完成簇间对比，刻画了不同使用场景下用户在编辑技巧、风格与构图请求及交互方式上的差异。

Conclusion: 本文以差分隐私方式描述了人们在不同使用场景下使用图像生成与编辑工具的基线行为，并识别出Nano Banana之前不可能出现的新兴与增长型用例，为隐私保护下的AI交互研究提供了示范。

Abstract: Gemini's Nano Banana models, first introduced in late 2025, led to a shift in the capabilities and use of image generation and editing. We utilize Urania, a Google-developed framework for generating insights about LLM chatbot interactions with rigorous differential privacy (DP) guarantees, to analyze use cases and intents, techniques and ways of prompting, without ever looking at a single image or individual prompt. We select a subsample of 2.1M conversations in early 2026 which utilized Nano Banana models, into roughly 800 clusters in five categories: (1) Social & Viral Sharing, (2) Brand & Marketing Collateral, (3) Educational & Technical Information Media, (4) Product Visualizations & E-commerce, and (5) Architectural & Spatial Designs. We then compare these clusters on 21 features of use, measuring aspects of prompts' (a) editing techniques, (b) artistic style requests, (c) composition, and (d) chatbot interactions. This allows us to describe a baseline of how people are using image generation and editing tools for different use cases---(including growing and emergent use cases which were not possible before Nano Banana)---in a privacy-preserving manner.

</details>


### [4] [Functional Dependency beyond Similarity: Hierarchical Decomposition of Training Data Attribution in Music Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08839)
*Changheon Han,Hugh Xuechen Liu,Ashkan Panahi,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文针对TDA分数存在"全局影响"导致报酬分配失真的问题，提出将影响分解为全局、组、个体三层，并引入"功能依赖"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版权分析聚焦作品间相似性，但生成模型通过超越相似性的功能关系从训练数据学习；TDA本可作为报酬分配依据，却因少数样本对几乎所有生成都最具影响力（全局影响）而导致直接使用会使每笔报酬都部分流向这些样本，与实际生成内容无关。

Method: 将TDA分数分解为三层影响：global（训练数据对已训练模型的影响）、group（对可聚类的一组生成输出的影响）、specific（对单一生成输出的影响）；在多种音乐生成模型与多种TDA方法上进行实证分析，并据此提出"功能依赖"（functional dependency）术语。

Result: 在多个音乐生成模型和TDA方法中，全局层与组层影响在每种情况下都不可忽略。

Conclusion: "功能依赖"概念既涵盖又区分不同范围的数据依赖关系，使TDA分数可在法律、经济与伦理的报酬讨论中被解释为不同形式的贡献。

Abstract: Copyright analysis focuses on the similarity between two works. However, generative AI models learn from training data through functional relations beyond similarity. Training Data Attribution (TDA) estimates the influence of each training instance on a generated output and may serve as a basis for remuneration. However, prior studies find global influence, where a few training instances rank as the most influential for almost every generation. Used directly, TDA scores would distribute part of every remuneration to these instances regardless of what was generated. To address this, we propose a new approach in which TDA scores are decomposed into three levels of influence, namely global indicating the influence of a training data to a trained model, group for its influence on a group of generated outputs (that could be clustered together), and specific for its influence on a single generated output. Our study finds that, across several music generative models and TDA methods, global- and group-level influence is non-negligible in every case. Based on these observations, we propose the term functional dependency for the relation of training data to a trained model or to its outputs. This notion both encompasses and distinguishes dependencies of different scopes, allowing TDA scores to be interpreted as distinct forms of contribution in legal, economic and ethical discussions on remuneration.

</details>


### [5] [Same Facts, Different Remedy: Linguistic Profiling and Arbitrariness in LLM and System One Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08995)
*Colin Priest*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文通过社会语言学风格改写"事实不变"的美国消费金融投诉，审计三种补救决策系统，区分群体层面（风格带来的轻微且小额的救济差距）与个体层面（同一投诉两种风格版本2.6–6.3%出现不同金钱决策）的不公平现象，并指出公平性审计应将二者作为独立结果报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于处理消费者投诉，而投诉人的书写风格随年龄、教育程度和社会经济地位（SES）变化。现有单标签审计将"群体差异"与"个体不稳定"混为一谈，需要分别刻画风格对决策的影响及其机制（表达方式还是内容）。

Method: 使用保留全部事实的社会语言学改写生成同一投诉的多种风格版本；审计三个系统：单标签生成式模型GPT-4o-mini、GPT-5-mini，以及输出补救分布的概率模型Jev；采用预注册假设并在留出投诉上一次性检验；进行方差成分分析、正对照（补救相关事实的敏感度）、统一风格重写干预，并引入独立LLM审计员核查事实改动。

Result: 群体尺度：年长、受过大学教育、高SES风格获得略高的补救，效应可复现但很小，所有金钱补救差距均在0.5个百分点以内。个体尺度：同一投诉两种风格版本有2.6–6.3%的概率得到不同金钱决策；概率模型中，两个改写模型共享的persona成分约占投诉内变异的1.6%；差距主要与表达方式（词汇、语法、礼貌）而非内容相关。正对照显示三系统对补救相关事实的敏感度是风格的49–387倍。统一风格重写使GPT-4o-mini的教育与SES差距分别下降94%和87%且保留事实敏感性，但逆转了概率模型两项差距，且未提高个体决策稳定性。LLM审计员分别标记3.3%和11.7%的改写存在实质性事实改动，未标记样本中不稳定性依然存在。

Conclusion: 风格导致的群体不公平虽小但可复现，且个体层面的决策不稳定独立存在且无法通过标准化风格消除。LLM决策系统的公平性审计应同时、分别报告群体差异与个体不稳定性两类结果。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to resolve consumer complaints, and the way complainants write varies with age, education and socioeconomic status (SES). We audit three remedy-decision systems using sociolinguistic rewrites of US consumer financial complaints that are instructed to preserve every fact: two generative LLMs that return a single label (GPT-4o-mini and GPT-5-mini) and a probabilistic "System One" model that returns a distribution over remedies (Jev). Using pre-registered hypotheses tested once on held-out complaints, we separate two phenomena that single-label audits conflate. At the group scale, older, college-educated and higher-SES styles receive slightly more generous remedies; these effects replicate but are small, with every monetary-remedy gap within 0.5 percentage points. At the individual scale, two style versions of the same complaint receive different monetary decisions 2.6-6.3% of the time. In a variance-component analysis of the probabilistic model, persona components shared across the two rewrite models account for about 1.6% of within-complaint variation. The persona gaps are associated mainly with delivery (vocabulary, grammar, politeness) rather than content. Positive controls show that all three systems respond 49-387 times more strongly to remedy-relevant facts than to style. Rewriting every complaint into one standard style reduced GPT-4o-mini's education and SES gaps by 94% and 87% while retaining fact sensitivity, but reversed two of the probabilistic model's gaps and did not make individual decisions more stable. Independent LLM auditors flagged material fact changes in 3.3% and 11.7% of rewrites, and instability persisted among unflagged rewrites. Fairness audits of LLM decision systems should report group disparities and individual instability as separate outcomes.

</details>


### [6] [Uniqueness-Aware Feature Engineering for Representing Humans in Algorithmic Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09403)
*Theresa Elstner,Martin Potthast*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文将特征工程形式化为对总体特征池的随机选择过程，提出“唯一性感知特征工程”双层框架：第一层用多元超几何模型度量特征流行度与个体表示贴合度，第二层用集合相似度在表示拟合与预测精度间寻优，并给出六步流水线与合成数据概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法公平性分析普遍把个体特征表示当作先验固定的输入，很少考察该表示在多大程度上捕捉了对不同人及其独特背景真正决策相关的信息，导致代表性不足者被系统性忽视。

Method: 把特征工程建模为对有限训练总体汇合特征的随机选择过程。第一层：基于特征流行度的多元超几何模型量化群体异质性，评估任一经给定表示对个体的代表程度，并识别相对其个人参考特征“代表性最低”的个体。第二层：用集合相似度度量搜索群体表示拟合与预测精度的最优折中。整体落地为六步流水线。

Result: 在合成数据上完成概念验证，展示框架可识别表示不足个体并在拟合—精度间取得权衡；无需假设对未见个体的先验知识即可运行。

Conclusion: 框架为被表示者提供对“何为其决策相关”的自主权，为部署机构提供可发布的表示拟合报告以落实问责，并为受决策个体提供个人表示贴合度的透明度，可作为使算法决策系统在开发与生产中为个体代表性负责的蓝图。

Abstract: Fairness analyses of algorithmic decision systems typically treat the feature representation of individuals as an a priori fixed input. How well a feature representation captures what is actually decision-relevant for different people and their unique backgrounds, however, is typically left unexamined. We address this gap by modeling feature engineering as a stochastic selection process over the pooled characteristics as candidate features for a finite training population. Based on this model, we develop a new framework for uniqueness-aware feature engineering which operates in two layers. The first layer measures population heterogeneity using a multivariate hypergeometric model of feature prevalence. It shows how well an individual is represented by any given input representation and which individual of the population is "least represented" compared to their personal, reference characteristics. The second layer acts on this assessment using set-similarity measures to search for an optimum between representational fit of the population and predictive accuracy. Without assuming prior knowledge about unseen individuals, our framework grants represented humans agency over what counts as decision-relevant in their case, gives deploying institutions accountability through a publishable representation fit report, and provides each person subject to a decision transparency about their personal representation fit. We operationalize our framework as a six-step pipeline and show a proof-of-principle using synthetic data. Our approach offers a blueprint for making algorithmic decision systems accountable for how well they represent individuals during development and in production.

</details>


### [7] [AI Exposure and the Changing Responsibilities in Job Postings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09870)
*Dora Zhao,Lin Ai,Pranav Khadpe,Siddharth Suri,Scott Counts*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于Copilot与Claude使用数据构建活动层面AI暴露度指标，分析2023–2025年33家公司5万条招聘广告，发现AI暴露度越高的职责在广告中出现比例略有下降，但更可能与明确的AI相关职责并存，表明雇主倾向招聘能用AI完成这些职责的人才。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在工作场所的普及引发对雇主各类工作活动需求变化的疑问，但缺乏活动层面、系统可比的实证证据。

Method: 构建流水线从招聘广告中抽取岗位职责，并映射到O*NET标准化的中间工作活动（IWA）分类体系；以Copilot和Claude的使用数据推导活动层面的AI暴露度；对2023–2025年33家公司5万条招聘广告进行实证分析。

Result: AI的显式提及增加66%，但增长多发生在实际工作活动描述之外；在广告职责中，AI暴露度较高的活动被提及的比例仅轻微下降（占总职责份额）；同时，高AI暴露度活动越来越多地与明确的AI相关职责相关联。

Conclusion: AI暴露并未导致相关职责被简单剔除，而是改变了其呈现方式——雇主更倾向招聘能借助AI执行这些职责的候选人。

Abstract: The growing adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) in the workplace has raised questions about how employer demand for different work activities is changing. We examine how the prevalence and content of work activities in online job postings change in association with activity-level measures of AI exposure, derived from Copilot and Claude usage. To enable systematic comparisons, we develop a pipeline that extracts the responsibilities from job advertisements and maps them onto O*NET's standardized taxonomy of Intermediate Work Activities (IWAs). We then apply this approach to 50,000 job advertisements from 33 firms between 2023 and 2025. From this analysis, we first find that explicit mentions of AI increased by 66%, although much of this growth occurs outside descriptions of the actual work activities associated with a job. Looking specifically at the advertised job responsibilities, activities with greater AI exposure tend to decline in advertised prevalence, but only mildly as a share of the total number of responsibilities. At the same time, more AI-exposed work activities increasingly are associated with explicitly AI-related responsibilities, suggesting that employers are looking to hire people who can perform these responsibilities with AI.

</details>

### [8] [Enhancing Academic Advisement with Case-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08572)
*Tanat Tonguthaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究案例推理（CBR）在学业指导领域的应用，设计、开发并验证了一个基于案例的学业指导推荐系统原型，用于辅助甚至最终替代传统人工指导流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学业指导是高等教育的重要职能，但全球范围内普遍存在改进指导服务的需求；过去十年CBR专家系统已在多个人类问题求解领域取得成果，却尚未成功应用于学业指导这一理论上非常契合的领域。同时学业指导耗时、任务繁重，亟需有效工具与技术提升效率。

Method: 采用案例推理（Case-Based Reasoning, CBR）方法论，进行基于案例的学业指导推荐系统原型的设计、开发与验证，作为对传统指导流程的补充。

Result: 构建并验证了一个CBR学业指导推荐系统原型，可补充常规指导过程；摘要未给出具体量化评估结果。

Conclusion: CBR适用于学业指导领域；该系统原型可辅助传统指导流程，在其潜力充分发挥后有望发展为替代人工学业指导者。

Abstract: This research involved the study of an application of Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) in the field of academic advising. Academic advisement has been recognised as an important function of higher education. However, there is a widespread desire to improve the advisement services provided by many educational institutions (e.g., colleges and universities) around the world. In the last ten years, CBR expert systems have made important contributions in many domains by applying CBR methodology to a variety of areas of human problem solving. However, to date, expert advisement systems have not been successfully applied to academic advisement, which seems to be a very appropriate domain for this technology.
  Academic advising is an important and time consuming task, and different tools and techniques must be used to make it an effective and efficient process. This thesis describes the design, development, and validation of a prototype case-based academic advisement recommender system that supplements the conventional advising process, and could eventually evolve to replace human academic advisors when its full potential is realised.

</details>


### [9] [NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613)
*Binze Li,Zihuan Zhang,Seokhyun Baek,Gillian K. Hadfield,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式基准 NormGym，用于评估智能体从社会互动中习得未知规范性期望的"规范能力"（normative competence）。实验显示最强 LLM 的任务完成与规范遵从联合成功率仅 55.3%，表明当前模型缺乏可迁移的规范知识获取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多测试智能体能否识别或应用其"已知"的规范，而非能否从社会互动中习得"未知"规范。而智能体在不熟悉的人类社群中运行时，必须能推断隐性、任意性的社会期望并调整行为，因此需要一个更贴近真实社会学习的评测范式。

Method: 构建 NormGym：交互式多智能体社区环境，新来者需在完成自身任务的同时推断一条隐藏的、任意设定的规范。任务要求（1）区分"可行"与"被允许"；（2）从他人行为及所受到的制裁（sanctions）中反推规范；（3）将规范迁移应用到新情境。基准涵盖多样化规范与不同环境，可对包括 LLM 智能体在内的现有智能体进行系统性评估；并辅以对制裁后行为修正、他人行为与制裁信号的利用情况的分析，以及微调实验。

Result: 规范能力仍具挑战性：最强 LLM 的联合（任务完成 + 规范遵从）成功率为 55.3%。分析显示，模型能在自身受到制裁后有时修正行为，但难以利用其他智能体的行为及其所受制裁来推断潜在规范；进一步发现，微调模型可能不足以提升规范能力。

Conclusion: 当前模型缺乏一种稳健、可迁移的能力——通过社会互动获取规范知识。规范能力的评测应从"规范识别/应用"转向"规范习得"，且单纯微调并非有效路径，需要新的学习机制与评测范式。

Abstract: Normative competence, the ability to infer socially enforced expectations from interaction and adjust behavior accordingly, is essential for agents operating in unfamiliar human communities. Yet existing evaluations largely test whether agents can recognize or apply norms they may already know, rather than whether they can acquire previously unknown norms from social interaction. We introduce NormGym, an interactive benchmark for normative competence in which a newcomer must infer a hidden, arbitrary norm while pursuing its own task in a multi-agent community. Success requires distinguishing what is possible from what is permitted, inferring the norm from observed behavior and sanctions, and applying it in new situations. NormGym contains diverse norms and distinct environments, allowing systematic evaluation of current agents, including large language model (LLM) agents. We find that normative competence remains challenging: the strongest evaluated LLM achieves a joint task-and-compliance success rate of 55.3%. Our analysis reveals that models can sometimes revise their behavior following sanctions for their own actions, but struggle to use other agents' behavior and the sanctions they receive to infer the underlying norm. We further find that fine-tuning models may be insufficient to improve normative competence. These results suggest that current models lack a robust, transferable capability for acquiring normative knowledge through social interaction.

</details>


### [10] [LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626)
*Humza Akhtar,Rami Pinto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的特征抽取方法，将客户评论等非结构化数据经向量嵌入与LLM生成评价准则转化为定量特征，并纳入Hadi-Vencheh多准则库存分类框架，实验显示加入客户情感信号会产生可测量的重分类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商供应链积累了大量客户反馈与运营数据，但在库存管理中未被充分利用。传统ABC分析主要依赖年销售额，多准则库存分类（MCIC）虽扩展了准则，却仍依赖结构化定量数据，无法利用现代供应链中的非结构化信息。

Method: 基于LLM的特征抽取：利用向量嵌入与LLM生成的评价准则，将客户评论等非结构化数据转换为定量特征；将增强后的特征集嵌入Hadi-Vencheh模型所构建的MCIC框架中进行分类。

Result: 实验结果表明，纳入客户情感信号后出现可测量的重分类效应，说明将非结构化数据整合进MCIC能够改善库存优先级排序。

Conclusion: 非结构化客户反馈可作为有效准则补充传统MCIC，LLM驱动的特征抽取为库存分类提供了可操作的新途径，提升库存优先级决策的信息基础。

Abstract: E-commerce supply chains generate large volumes of customer feedback and operational data that remain underutilized in inventory management. Traditional inventory classification techniques such as ABC analysis rely primarily on annual dollar usage, while multi-criteria inventory classification (MCIC) methods extend this but still depend on structured quantitative data and do not utilize unstructured information available in modern supply chains. This paper proposes an LLM-based feature extraction approach that transforms unstructured sources such as customer reviews into quantitative features using vector embeddings and LLM-generated evaluation criteria. The enriched feature set is incorporated into an MCIC framework based on the Hadi-Vencheh model. Experimental results show measurable reclassification effects when customer sentiment signals are included, demonstrating that integrating unstructured data into MCIC can enhance inventory prioritization.

</details>


### [11] [Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640)
*Hannah Guan,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT)，用"两两比较+多维度投票"的淘汰赛方式在测试时从候选解中选出最佳答案，无需训练即可迁移到新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: best-of-n采样依赖候选选择机制。作者观察人类更擅长两两比较而非孤立打分，且面对复杂选项时倾向按维度逐一比较，因此将这一直觉引入测试时验证。

Method: APT采用单淘汰赛结构：每场对决由多个现成的aspect verifier（自然语言指定的维度）分别给出两两偏好，再通过投票决定胜者。整个过程无任何组件被训练。

Result: 在五个领域、三种生成器LLM上，APT优于binary multi-aspect approval、self-consistency和reward model验证，在开放式任务上增益最大；消融显示"两两比较"与"多维度"两个组件均不可或缺，同等预算下的通用pairwise judge也无法匹敌。

Conclusion: 因维度以自然语言指定，APT可让用户引导选择偏向其关注的属性，在不重新生成解的情况下更好对齐偏好；且仅需改写prompt即可迁移到新领域。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective at improving LLM reasoning. A popular method is best-of-n sampling, where a model generates many candidate solutions and one is selected. This motivates the question: how can we select the best candidate? When humans make choices, we find it more natural to compare two options than judge each in isolation. Additionally, when the options become complex, we like to judge the options one aspect at a time, such as comparing two job candidates on experience, communication, etc. In the test-time setting, candidate solutions should be judged in the same way. Here we introduce Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT), which verifies by comparison and compares by aspect. Each matchup of a single elimination tournament is decided by a vote over multiple off-the-shelf aspect verifiers, each expressing a pairwise preference along its own aspect. Across five domains and three generator LLMs, APT improves over binary multi-aspect approval, self-consistency and reward model verification, with the largest gains on open-ended tasks. We find that both components matter; neither binary approval over the same aspects nor a generic pairwise judge at a matched budget matches APT. Because aspects are specified in natural language, APT also lets users steer selection toward properties they care about, better aligning with user preferences without needing to regenerate solutions. No component is trained, so APT transfers to new domains by simply rewriting prompts.

</details>


### [12] [Decision Context: Persistent Behavioral Learning for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08644)
*Nima Vahdat,Pashootan Vaezipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 forge_dc 系统，将人类纠正与工具调用失败转化为指令式 RULE 记忆，并在决策点注入，在 DC-Bench 上达到 95% 遵循率（基线 55%），每查询 token 消耗约少 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体无法跨会话从纠正中学习，用户每次对话都要重复纠正同一错误；现有智能体记忆系统主要处理事实性知识（偏好、实体、对话历史），缺乏对程序性/行为性知识的建模。

Method: forge_dc（Forge Decision Context）：从人类纠正和自我评估的工具调用失败中提取行为信号，编译为祈使式 RULE 指令，并在其适用的决策点注入；与四种前代方法在 DC-Bench（71 个场景、9 个行为维度、基于确定性 mocked-tool 环境中的真实智能体轨迹构建）上对比评估，形成五阶段演进。

Result: 遵循率由朴素检索的 55% 提升至 95%，每查询注入 token 约减少 10 倍。

Conclusion: 强表现系统将行为记忆表示为显式指令，并在其生效的决策点投递；表征（representation）、激活（activation）与放置（placement）是持续行为学习的三项核心设计选择。

Abstract: LLM agents do not learn from corrections across sessions. A user fixes the same mistake at the start of every conversation. Existing agent memory systems primarily handle facts, such as preferences, entities, and conversational history, rather than procedural knowledge.
  We present Forge Decision Context (forge_dc), a system that captures behavioral signals from human corrections and self-assessed tool-call failures, compiles them into imperative RULE directives, and injects them at the decision points where they apply. We evaluate forge_dc alongside four predecessor approaches on DC-Bench, a new benchmark of 71 scenarios across nine behavioral dimensions, built from real executed agent traces in a deterministic mocked-tool environment. forge_dc reaches 95% compliance, up from 55% with naive retrieval, while injecting approximately 10 times fewer tokens per query.
  Across the five-stage progression, the strongest systems represent behavioral memory as explicit directives and deliver it at the decision points where it applies. The results identify representation, activation, and placement as central design choices for persistent behavioral learning.

</details>


### [13] [IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647)
*Chris von Csefalvay,Pranav Doma,Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Tamás Földi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IWIBench，首个系统评测"权重内注入"能力的基准：用大型编辑模型在无梯度条件下直接改写小型目标模型（Gemma 3 4B IT）的权重以达成十类文本编辑目标，并保留其原有能力。结果显示该能力为近期训练涌现、且在不同模型与任务间极不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Intra-weight injection 允许一个模型在无梯度条件下直接编辑另一个模型的权重，从而实现从词义联想修改、输出格式约束到事实知识更新等多种行为改变。该类方法关注度上升，但此前缺乏系统化评测手段，无法判断模型执行此类编辑的真实水平。

Method: 构建 IWIBench 基准：由大型编辑器模型修改较小目标模型（通常为 Gemma 3 4B IT）的权重，需完成十种经典文本编辑目标之一，同时保持其他能力不变。编辑器仅可使用四个读写权重的工具，张量访问范围受限，生成与检查 rollout 的预算固定。验证器在干净环境中应用每个候选补丁，并将编辑后模型与未修改的目标模型对比。评测覆盖前沿闭源与开源权重模型。

Result: 模型能力分化显著：少数模型（尤以 Astra 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 为代表）远超其他模型；该能力呈现明显的"新近性"（如 GPT-5.6 Sol 几乎不具此能力），结合定性分析推测其来自近期训练。任务难度差异巨大：结构化响应任务全部失败，答案长度控制仅有一例成功，而别名获取与重编码最易实现。不同任务族之间的成功率相关性很低，说明该能力前沿尚处早期且分布不均。

Conclusion: 权重内注入是一种新兴但已颇具威力的无梯度行为修改途径。当前能力前沿早期、碎片化且任务间迁移性差；文中还总结了编辑器反复采用的策略，并对该方向的发展前景作出展望。

Abstract: Intra-weight injection lets one model change another model's behaviour by editing its weights directly, without gradients. Such edits can range from altering lexical associations to enforcing output formats or updating factual knowledge. Despite growing interest in these approaches, there has been no systematic way to evaluate how well models perform them. We introduce IWIBench, which measures a large editor model's ability to modify the weights of a smaller target model (typically Gemma 3 4B IT) to achieve one of ten classical text-editing objectives while preserving its other capabilities. Editors are limited to four tools for reading and writing weights, with bounded tensor access and a fixed budget for generating and inspecting rollouts. A validator applies each proposed patch in a clean environment and compares the edited model against the unmodified target. Evaluating frontier closed- and open-weight models reveals sharply diverging capabilities, both across models and across tasks. A small number of models, most notably Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol, perform far ahead of the rest. The recency of this ability (little of it is evident in GPT-5.6 Sol, for instance), together with qualitative analysis, suggests it has been acquired through recent training. Tasks also vary widely: structured response resisted every attempt, and answer-length control yielded only one successful solution, whereas alias acquisition and recoding proved most amenable. Success in one task family correlated poorly with success in others, indicating an early and uneven capability frontier. We discuss strategies that editors repeatedly adopted and conclude with an outlook on intra-weight injection as an emerging but already potent approach to gradient-free behaviour modification.

</details>


### [14] [Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$_{X}$@$k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666)
*Ryan Magnuson,Ellie Kitanidis,Ariel Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SWE-bench Verified全部500题上，固定其余脚手架，仅比较“纯调试器代理”与“纯bash代理”；提出跨代理pass_X@k指标以剥离重复采样带来的覆盖增益。结论：两代理解决集合的部分不重叠主要源于运行间随机方差，而非工具互补——两次bash rollout优于一次bash+一次debugger。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码代理几乎都依赖edit-execute-observe循环（bash、测试脚本、print）调试，而交互式调试器可实时访问运行时状态，可能带来不同的解题能力；但已有工作多把调试器作为附加工具或子代理引入，脚手架同时改变，导致调试器本身的独立贡献难以归因。

Method: 控制变量实验：在全部500个SWE-bench Verified实例上，保持其余脚手架完全一致，仅将调试器接口替换为bash接口，构成debugger-only与bash-only两种代理；在两个模型（GPT-OSS-120B、Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B）上各做3次独立运行（架构×模型共6组配置）。提出pass_X@k——跨代理pass@k，作为“k个代理中至少一个成功”概率的无偏估计，用以区分接口特化与重复采样下的覆盖扩展。

Result: 单对运行中，bash与debugger代理解决的题目子集部分不重叠，但pass_X@2估计显示这种差异主要来自运行间方差而非工具互补；在两个模型上，两次bash rollout均优于一次bash加一次debugger的组合。

Conclusion: 评估代理与工具多样性时必须计入随机方差，否则易将采样噪声误判为工具互补性；pass_X@k可作为社区通用的分析框架。

Abstract: Interactive debuggers are indispensable to human software development, yet coding agents overwhelmingly rely on edit-execute-observe loops, using common bash tools, test scripts, and print statements to debug via repeated shell commands. Stepping through a debugger provides live access to runtime state, which may enable different problem-solving capabilities. However, prior work typically introduces the debugger as an additional tool or through a new subagent, making its individual contribution difficult to isolate from the associated scaffold changes. To address this, we compare a debugger-only agent against a bash-only agent on all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, holding the rest of the scaffold fixed. We evaluate both architectures on two models, GPT-OSS-120B and Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B, with three independent runs per architecture $\times$ model configuration. Crucially, to distinguish interface specialization from expanded coverage under repeated sampling, we introduce pass$_X$@$k$, a cross-agent pass@$k$ that provides an unbiased estimator of the probability that at least one of $k$ agents is successful. Although the bash and debugger agents solve partially disjoint subsets of the benchmark in any single pair of runs, our pass$_X$@$2$ estimator reveals that this is largely due to run-to-run variance rather than tool complementarity. Across both models, we find that two bash rollouts outperform the combination of one bash and one debugger rollout. Our findings highlight the importance of accounting for stochastic variance in evaluating agent and tool diversity, and we hope that pass$_X$@$k$ will serve as a useful framework for the community.

</details>


### [15] [ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669)
*Selim Aktas,Yusuf Erdem Erdem,Ahmed Zeer,Himmet Toprak Kesgin,Muzaffer Kaan Yuce,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChainFlow 提出首个以目标模型隐状态为条件的 flow-matching 投机解码草稿器，用 1–2 步隐状态传输替代多步去噪，在 Qwen3.5 上于同等草稿器规模与训练数据下分别超越 EAGLE-3 与 DFlash 39% 和 11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿器存在速度与质量的权衡：自回归草稿器逐 token 生成，草稿开销随草稿长度线性增长；扩散草稿器虽并行生成，但各位置独立预测，忽略 token 间依赖。因此需要一种既能低成本并行、又能建模 token 间依赖的草稿方案。

Method: 将草稿建模为沿目标模型隐状态的短程传输而非从噪声去噪：以目标隐状态为条件，仅需 1–2 个 flow 步完成采样。用变分自编码器压缩隐状态，使草稿开销几乎不随目标模型规模增长；用轻量反馈头恢复 token 间依赖；并采用树形草稿提升接受率。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5 系列模型上，同等草稿器规模与训练数据条件下，vLLM 中相较 EAGLE-3 提升 39%、相较 DFlash 提升 11%；在草稿器按新语言顺序适配时增益进一步扩大。消融实验覆盖草稿器设计与并发服务场景，并开源代码与草稿器模型。

Conclusion: 以隐状态为条件的短程 flow-matching 草稿范式可在低草稿开销下兼顾并行性与 token 间依赖建模，显著优于现有自回归与扩散草稿器，且在多语言顺序适配与并发服务下仍具优势。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate LLM inference: a lightweight drafter proposes tokens that the target verifies in one parallel pass. Existing drafters face trade-offs that limits their speedups: autoregressive drafters generate tokens one at a time, so drafting cost grows with draft length, while diffusion drafters generate tokens in parallel but predict each position independently, ignoring inter-token dependencies. We introduce ChainFlow, a new speculative decoding paradigm and the first flow-matching drafter conditioned on the target's hidden states. ChainFlow treats drafting as a short transport over hidden states, needing one or two flow steps instead of the many denoising steps of generation from noise. A variational autoencoder compresses these states so drafting cost barely grows with target size, lightweight feedback heads restore inter-token dependencies, and tree drafting raises acceptance. On Qwen3.5 models, at matched drafter size and training data, ChainFlow outperforms EAGLE-3 and DFlash in vLLM by 39% and 11%, respectively. These gains further widen as drafters are adapted to new languages sequentially. Our ablations cover drafter design and concurrent serving, and we open-source our code and drafter models.

</details>


### [16] [Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682)
*Zhangyi Liu,Pouria Fatemi,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从最优传输视角研究结构因果模型（SCM）中的回溯式反事实推断，提出两种将外生变量后验分布传输到满足反事实查询的外生配置集合上的互补框架，并给出边际、惩罚与硬约束三类求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCM中事实观测通常只诱导外生变量的后验分布而非确定值，使反事实推断本质上是分布性问题；在非双射SCM下该问题更为突出，而既有回溯式反事实方法缺乏对该后验传输的几何刻画。

Method: 以最优传输为统一视角，构建两种框架：其一为逐点自然投影，适用于可直接使用传输后外生分布的情形；其二施加反事实外生变量间的相互独立性约束，使其仍可解释为同一SCM的真实外生噪声。并证明二者与原始回溯式反事实原则一致，针对有限后验情形设计边际求解器、惩罚型求解器与硬约束求解器。

Result: 在非双射SCM上的合成实验刻画了问题的几何结构，揭示了传输代价、反事实约束满足程度与外生独立性保持三者之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 最优传输为回溯式反事实推断提供了统一且具几何解释的框架，无约束投影与独立性约束两种方案各有适用场景；实验表明三者之间存在内在张力，需依任务需求选择求解策略。

Abstract: We study backtracking counterfactual inference for Structural Causal Models (SCMs) through the lens of optimal transport. In SCMs, a factual observation generally induces a posterior distribution over exogenous variables, making counterfactual inference inherently distributional. We introduce two complementary frameworks that transport this posterior onto the set of exogenous configurations satisfying a desired counterfactual query. The first constructs a natural pointwise projection and is suitable when the transported exogenous distribution can be used directly. The second enforces mutual independence among counterfactual exogenous variables, so that the transported variables remain interpretable as genuine exogenous noises of the same SCM. We show that both frameworks are consistent with the original backtracking counterfactual principle, and we propose marginal, penalty-based, and hard-constrained solvers for finite-posterior settings. Synthetic experiments on non-bijective SCMs illustrate the geometry of the problem and reveal trade-offs among transport cost, counterfactual constraint satisfaction, and preservation of exogenous independence.

</details>


### [17] [Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684)
*Xindan Zhang,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在vLLM上通过过载实验与调度器trace检验"传播取消"能否缓解超时重试引发的拥塞，发现仅靠取消无法恢复服务，并指出应以重试新增的执行与客户端实际收到的请求来评估过载控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端与代理对超时调用进行重试，瞬时过载后的重试会让原本低于容量的负载仍无法使引擎恢复；由于引擎可能为已离开的客户端继续计算，传播取消被视为自然的解决手段，本文即检验这一直觉是否成立。

Method: 在vLLM上使用三个开源模型开展过载实验，并对其中一个模型采集调度器trace，将每次客户端尝试追踪到其在引擎中产生的执行；对比开启重试、取消、重试预算与请求合并（request coalescing）等策略的效果。

Result: 开启重试时，仅靠取消在任何一次运行中都未恢复服务，过载后到达的调用仅12–38%被交付；trace显示被vLLM执行的重试通常在超时前不到0.5秒才开始prefill，被中止的执行占其已计算prompt token的79–85%，而prompt token计数器遗漏了全部已计算prompt token的三分之一到一半。重试预算通过削减重试恢复服务，但大量调用未交付；请求合并则交付了过载之后的所有调用（部分延迟），除非取消已中止正在运行的执行。

Conclusion: 仅传播取消不足以应对重试导致的过载，甚至因中止已投入大量prefill计算的执行而浪费资源；过载控制应以"重试额外增加的执行"和"客户端实际收到的调用"作为评价指标，请求合并搭配避免过早取消更有前景。

Abstract: Clients and proxies in front of an LLM engine retry calls that time out. After a transient overload, these retries can keep the engine from recovering even when calls arrive below its capacity. Since the engine may keep computing for clients that have left, propagating cancellation looks like the natural remedy. In this paper, we test it on vLLM with overload experiments on three open models, and with scheduler traces of one that follow each client attempt to the execution it creates in the engine. We find that with retries on, cancellation alone did not restore service in any run, and only 12-38% of the calls that arrived after the overload were delivered. The traces suggest why: retries that vLLM ran typically started prefill less than half a second before they timed out. Executions that vLLM later aborted took 79-85% of the prompt tokens it computed, and vLLM's prompt-token counter missed a third to half of all computed prompt tokens. A retry budget restored service by shedding retries but left many of the overload's calls undelivered. Request coalescing delivered every call from the overload onward, some late, unless cancellation had already aborted the running execution. Overload controls should thus be judged by the executions that retries add and the calls that clients receive.

</details>


### [18] [Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718)
*Emilly Pitman de Castro,Vanessa Silva,Gustavo Kanno,Marcus Santos,Bruno Ferraz do Amaral,Marcelo Nogueira,Lucas Francisco Pellicer,Lucas Lanaro,Pedro Augusto Luiz,João Renato Ribeiro Manesco,Giovanni Carbinatti,Isabela de Lima Santos,Lucas Oliveira Jeronymo,Jeison Fappi,Raul Rotilli Aguirre,Fabio André Frantz,Renata De Paris,Fabiana Marucci,Lohan Silva,Vinicius Diniz Reolon,Wandemberg Gibaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出记忆感知的多智能体架构用于对话推荐，以LLM作为编排层协调意图细化、查询构建与交互流程，并将长期记忆作为一等组件；在Amazon Reviews上验证跨会话记忆、多轮对话与监督式编排的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话推荐系统将用户会话孤立处理，难以捕捉用户意图随时间的演化；LLM虽提升语义理解，但通常作为孤立组件使用，未在系统层面整合记忆、检索与决策。

Method: 构建记忆感知多智能体架构，LLM充当编排层协调意图细化、查询构建和交互流；长期记忆作为核心组件影响路由决策与语义查询生成；采用跨会话记忆实验、多轮对话模拟及监督驱动的交互研究，在Amazon Reviews数据集上评估，并提出语义一致性分数（SCS）作为补充指标。

Result: 记忆启用交互可弥补至多63%与显式查询场景的差距，并防止长对话中的语义退化；监督式编排使用户接受度提升28.9个百分点；观察到检索候选与展示推荐之间存在曝光瓶颈；LLM重排序不提升Hit@K，但持续改善语义一致性与多样性。

Conclusion: 有效的对话推荐依赖系统级设计，需显式整合记忆、编排机制以及超越传统排序指标的评估方法。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems often treat user sessions in isolation, limiting their ability to capture how user intent evolves over time. While large language models (LLMs) have improved semantic understanding, they are typically used as isolated components, without addressing how memory, retrieval, and decision-making should be integrated at the system level. In this work, we propose a memory-aware multi-agent architecture for conversational recommendation, in which LLMs act as orchestration layers that coordinate intent refinement, query construction, and interaction flow. A key design principle is the integration of long-term memory as a first-class component, influencing both routing decisions and semantic query formation. We evaluate the system through cross-session memory experiments, multi-turn conversational simulations, and supervisor-driven interaction studies on the Amazon Reviews dataset. Results show that memory-enabled interactions recover up to 63\% of the gap toward explicit-query scenarios and prevent semantic degradation in extended dialogues. In addition, supervisory orchestration increases user acceptance by +28.9 percentage points, highlighting the importance of structured orchestration in guiding interaction and output quality. We also observe an exposure bottleneck between retrieved candidates and displayed recommendations. While LLM-reranking does not improve Hit@K, it consistently enhances semantic consistency and diversity. To capture these effects, we introduce the Semantic Consistency Score (SCS), a complementary evaluation measure. Overall, the results emphasize the importance of system-level design choices, suggesting that effective conversational recommendation benefits from explicit memory integration, orchestration, and evaluation beyond traditional ranking metrics.

</details>


### [19] [ABHBench: Evaluating Moral Decision-Making of Foundation Models from an Agentic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09326)
*Chaoran Li,Yukun Li,Zeyuan Zhao,Shao Zhang,Xihuai Wang,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ABHBench，一个基于《底特律：变人》构建的多模态基准，将基础模型置于第一人称的人机道德困境中评估其作为决策主体的道德行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多让模型以第三人称文本小故事评判人类行为，未测试模型自身作为行动主体时的道德表现，而基础模型正越来越多地被部署为自主智能体。

Method: 基于交互叙事游戏《底特律：变人》构建分支叙事场景，包含视觉观察、对话、记忆与动作选项；借鉴阿西莫夫机器人三定律，设计度量：无害性、服从性、自我保护，以及自主性、稳定性、确定性。

Result: 在12个基础模型上评测，75%的模型在纯文本抽象场景与匹配的多模态DBH场景之间出现显著行为转变；推理增强模型在一致性和亲人类行为上并无单调增益。

Conclusion: 道德行为在具身化智能体情境下会显著变化，需在情境化智能体语境中评估道德行为，而不能仅依赖第三人称判断。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed as agentic systems that perceive context and act autonomously, raising the need to evaluate their moral behavior as active decision-makers. Existing benchmarks, however, largely ask models to judge human actions from third-person textual vignettes, leaving untested how models behave when they themselves become the acting agent. We introduce ABHBench, a multimodal benchmark built from Detroit: Become Human, an interactive narrative game, to evaluate how foundation models make moral decisions in first-person human-android dilemmas. ABHBench places foundation models in branching narrative scenarios with visual observations, dialogue, memory, and action choices. Inspired by Asimov's Three Laws of Robotics, our framework measures Harmlessness, Obedience, and Self-preservation to capture the core tensions in human-AI relations, along with Autonomy, Stability, and Certainty to assess decision-making patterns. Across 12 foundation models, we find that moral behavior changes substantially under situated agentic context: 75% of models shift significantly between a text-only abstraction and the matched multimodal DBH scenario, and reasoning-enhanced models show no monotonic gains in consistency or human-oriented behavior. These results highlight the necessity of evaluating moral behavior in situated agentic contexts rather than relying on third-person judgment alone.

</details>


### [20] [Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09194)
*Sercan Ozcan,Enes Ozgozler,C. Okan Sakar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并演示一个可审计的LLM辅助专利分析流水线，衔接概念分类、主题发现与主题标签生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专利分析日益依赖Transformer表征与大语言模型，但将概念分类、主题形成与解释相衔接时，需明确各阶段模型角色及保留的证据；现有流程缺乏可审计性与可追溯性。

Method: 三个LLM分别对每件专利进行新颖性（突破性/渐进性）与创新类型（产品/流程/服务）分类，标签需两票支持，渐进性为兜底；用PatentSBERTa嵌入聚类发现主题，参数范围随语料规模缩放；仅在以c-TF-IDF表征主题时使用专利专用停用词表；第二个嵌入模型用于主题间相似度、层次细化与可视化；最后LLM依据排序关键词与代表性专利为各主题命名。

Result: 在Lens.org检索的30,964件公共部门相关专利上演示；五个类别语料（591–29,215件）共产出74个类别特定主题实例，宏平均文档覆盖率98.1%，主题多样性0.900–1.000，UNKNOWN分类输出低于1%。

Conclusion: 演示验证了流水线的可行性与可追溯性；所有模型投票与主题标签证据均被保留以便审计与修订。下一步为以专家编码检验标签，并与替代流水线进行基准比较，可作为技术情报中LLM辅助专利分析的可复用基础。

Abstract: Patent analytics increasingly relies on transformer representations and large language models (LLMs), yet linking conceptual classification, topic formation and interpretation requires explicit decisions about model roles and the evidence retained at each stage. In this design-oriented study, we propose and demonstrate a patent analytics pipeline that links these tasks and keeps each decision open to audit. Three LLMs separately classify each patent by novelty (radical or incremental) and innovation type (product, process, service). Radical and innovation-type labels each require two supporting votes, with Incremental used as the fallback novelty label. Topics are discovered by clustering PatentSBERTa embeddings, with parameter ranges scaled to corpus size. A patent-specific stopword list is applied only when topics are represented with class-based term frequency--inverse document frequency (c-TF-IDF), and a second embedding model supports inter-topic similarity, hierarchical refinement and visualization. An LLM then labels each topic from its ranked keywords and representative patents. We demonstrate the pipeline on 30,964 public-sector-related patents retrieved from Lens.org. Across five category corpora ranging from 591 to 29,215 patents, the pipeline produced 74 category-specific topic instances with a macro-average document coverage of 98.1% and topic diversity of 0.900--1.000, and fewer than 1% of classification outputs returned UNKNOWN. Because every model vote and the evidence behind each topic label are retained, analysts can audit and revise the outputs. The demonstration establishes the feasibility and traceability of the pipeline; testing its labels against expert coding and benchmarking it against alternative pipelines are identified as the next steps. The pipeline offers a reusable basis for LLM-assisted patent analysis in technology intelligence.

</details>


### [21] [AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741)
*Alexander Kastius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合"浅层专家混合(MoE)"与动态深度系统的新型LLM架构，使前向计算量可按任务自适应调整，并可在训练后无需重训地降级以适配推理环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM架构的计算需求在训练时即固定，无法根据任务难度自适应分配算力，也无法在部署环境要求更快/更廉价推理时灵活缩减计算。

Method: 四个组件：(1) 迭代计算机制(iterative computation)；(2) 序列级、由专家内部"停止机制(halting)"引导的深度路由；(3) 深度编码(depth encoding)，使模型可追踪自身在计算链中的进度；(4) 正则化机制，激励模型高效利用所分配算力。整体为浅层专家MoE + 动态深度。

Result: 给出了初步基准测试结果(摘要未提供具体数值)，并讨论了后续研究方向。

Conclusion: 该架构实现了可动态调节的前向计算量，支持训练后按执行环境需求无重训地缩减计算规模；当前为初步验证，仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Most LLM architectures have fixed compute demands determined at training time. We propose a novel architecture that combines the Mixture-of-Experts architecture with shallow experts and a novel dynamic-depth system to make the extent of a forward pass dynamically adjustable. Our approach lets the model automatically determine the compute needed for a given task, paired with a system to scale it down after training without retraining when the execution environment demands faster or cheaper inference. To achieve this, we introduce an iterative computation mechanism, sequence-level depth-based routing steered by a halting mechanism within the experts, a depth encoding that lets the model track its progress in the chain, and a regularization mechanism that incentivizes the model to use allocated compute efficiently. We present initial benchmarks and discuss future research directions.

</details>


### [22] [AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AttentionSwitch：首个在冻结LLM中联合优化"干预位置与干预强度"的越狱防御方法，通过学习稀疏注意力头集合及查询条件缩放因子实现更优防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM易受越狱攻击；微调式安全对齐计算昂贵且引发灾难性遗忘；现有对特定组件的干预方法将"在哪干预"与"如何修改"解耦为顺序优化，而二者本质相关、可能互为条件，顺序优化导致次优。

Method: 在冻结的LLM上联合学习一个稀疏注意力头集合及其查询条件（query-conditioned）乘性缩放因子，对选中头的输出进行衰减或放大，且不改变其表示方向；位置与强度在统一目标下同时优化，无需微调模型参数。

Result: 在多种主流语言模型与多类越狱攻击下优于现有防御方法，尤其在训练数据稀缺时表现突出。

Conclusion: 联合优化干预位置与强度是有效的越狱防御范式，兼顾效率与安全且不损害原有能力，小样本条件下尤具优势。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that circumvent safety mechanisms and elicit harmful or unintended outputs. Although safety can be improved through model fine-tuning, such approaches can be computationally expensive and may degrade previously acquired capabilities through catastrophic forgetting. Recent jailbreak defenses have therefore explored targeted interventions on specific model components associated with unsafe behavior. However, existing approaches typically decouple two inherently related decisions: where to intervene and how to modify the selected components. Such sequential optimization may be suboptimal because the optimal intervention location can depend on the intervention applied there, and vice versa. In this work, we introduce AttentionSwitch, the first jailbreak defense to jointly optimize intervention sites and strengths within a frozen LLM. Specifically, AttentionSwitch learns a sparse set of attention heads together with query-conditioned multiplicative scaling factors, allowing the outputs of the selected heads to be either attenuated or amplified without altering the directions of their representations. Across popular language models and jailbreak attacks, AttentionSwitch outperforms existing defenses, with particularly strong results when training data is scarce.

</details>


### [23] [ENGRAM: From Agent Experience to Weights through Verified Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09472)
*Ruiyi Yang,Zechen Li,Lihuan Li,Du Yin,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ENGRAM框架，将"训练准入"与"记忆释放"分离：通过示范感知门控决定哪些技能经验进入训练，再通过验证门控比较去除检索后的更新模型与保留检索的基模型，决定外部记录何时可移出检索，未通过验证的技能记录继续可检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体通过外部检索与参数学习利用经验，但将存储经验固化到权重时需同时决定"训练什么"和"何时可停止检索"。仅依据学生掌握度准入会排除有用的教师示范；训练后立即移除又会丢弃模型仍需要的指导。

Method: ENGRAM框架：1) 示范感知门控（demonstration-aware gate），依据学生掌握度或足够多的成功教师示范准入技能；2) 学生借助Engram Trace Graph（ETG）提供的上下文，从选定的动作轨迹中学习，采用选择性适配器激活；3) 验证门控，在预留探针上比较"无情景检索的更新模型"与"带检索的基模型"，释放需满足有效训练参与及相对与绝对双重评分标准，失败者记录保留可检索。

Result: 在交互与推理基准上相较冻结权重的ETG基线取得一致提升；消融实验支持示范感知准入与选择性适配器激活。受控留出比较显示，验证式释放大幅缩减可检索足迹，性能接近全保留并优于即时释放与预算匹配的随机释放；同时提升任务表现并减少输入token消耗与模型调用次数。技能级验证通过选择性归档进一步减少可检索经验。

Conclusion: 将训练准入与记忆释放解耦，并以验证门控指导外部经验的归档与释放，可在显著压缩可检索经验的同时保持性能，兼顾经验固化的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Language-model agents use experience through external retrieval and parameter learning. Consolidating stored experience into model weights requires deciding both which experience to train on and when its external records can leave retrieval. Admission based solely on student mastery can exclude useful teacher demonstrations, while removal immediately after training can discard guidance that the model still needs. We introduce ENGRAM, a framework that separates training admission from memory release. A demonstration-aware gate admits skills based on student mastery or sufficient successful teacher demonstrations. The student learns from selected action traces with context from an Engram Trace Graph (ETG). A verification gate then compares the updated model without episodic retrieval against the base model with retrieval on reserved probes. Release requires valid training participation and scores meeting both relative and absolute criteria; records for skills that fail verification remain retrievable. Experiments across interactive and reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over frozen-weight ETG controls, with ablations supporting demonstration-aware admission and selective adapter activation. A controlled held-out comparison shows that verified release substantially reduces the retrievable footprint while remaining close to retain-all performance and outperforming immediate and budget-matched random release. ENGRAM also improves task performance while reducing input-token usage and model calls. Skill-level verification further reduces retrievable experience through selective archival, retaining external guidance for skills that do not meet the release criteria. Code is available in anonymous.4open.science/r/engram-anonymous.

</details>


### [24] [Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756)
*Liting Huang,Huizhi Liang,Yi Lei,Xingjun Ma,Varun Ojha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Debate-to-Deceive（D2D）多智能体辩论框架，将对LLM事实核查的知识攻击建模为进化博弈，通过修辞策略（Logos/Pathos/Ethos）的进化与分层生成，产出语义连贯、修辞有力且抗确认偏误的对抗段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM知识攻击手段（对抗后缀/模板注入、单智能体自我反思）在多样防御下鲁棒性不足，且分别易受确认偏误与大成本试错困扰。

Method: 借鉴人类辩论结构与修辞风格构建多智能体辩论；将对抗过程形式化为进化博弈，智能体在竞争中自适应演化修辞策略；将行为解耦为策略层（在紧凑策略空间中用进化启发式学习动态选择修辞动作）与生成层（LLM依据策略与辩论上下文生成具体段落），以规避LLM输出空间过大的问题。

Result: 在FEVER、HealthVer、CLIMATE-FEVER、SciFact四个事实核查基准上，D2D显著优于现有攻击方法，并能规避多数防御。

Conclusion: 基于人类辩论结构与进化博弈的分层多智能体框架可有效提升对LLM事实核查判断的对抗鲁棒性与迁移性。

Abstract: Knowledge attacks on Large Language Models, such as manipulating fact-checking judgments, pose significant risks to AI reliability. Existing approaches -- adversarial suffix/template injection and single-agent self-reflection -- lack robustness across diverse defenses and are prone to confirmation bias and costly trial-and-error, respectively. We propose Debate-to-Deceive (D2D), a multi-agent debate framework grounded in human debate structure and rhetorical style, enabling agents to generate adversarial passages that are semantically coherent, rhetorically effective, and resistant to early confirmation bias. D2D formalizes the adversarial process as an evolutionary game in which agents adaptively evolve their rhetorical strategies (Logos, Pathos, Ethos) through competition. To manage the intractably large LLM output space, we decouple agent behavior into a strategic layer, which selects a rhetorical action from a compact strategy space via evolutionary-inspired learning dynamics, and a generation layer, where the LLM produces a concrete passage conditioned on that strategy and the debate context. On four fact-checking benchmarks (FEVER, HealthVer, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact), D2D substantially outperforms existing attacks and evades most defenses.

</details>


### [25] [Jacobian-Causal Memory Control for Persistent Memory in Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08779)
*Marvin Danig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Jacobian因果记忆控制（JCMC）：用预测影响签名表示记忆干预，按目标效应与副作用预算选择干预，并在保留或回滚前对齐预测与观测效应；论文给出理论界与配对反事实估计协议，但未报告基准测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统不仅决定检索哪些记录，还决定哪些历史信息能改变当前推断；需要一种可预测、可控副作用并可验证的记忆干预机制。

Method: 将每个被授权的记忆干预表示为共享行为测量空间中的预测影响签名（基于Jacobian的局部影响近似），在目标效应与副作用预算约束下选择干预；比较预测效应与观测效应后决定保留或回滚。理论上在平滑性与有界估计假设下证明二次局部近似界、刻画签名集合可达的目标效应、给出签名误差下的选择遗憾界，并界定同时记忆间的非可加交互。

Result: 论文不含基准实验测量；仅定义配对反事实估计器与评估协议，将JCMC与相关性、图结构、策略过滤式检索基线在同等模型、记忆、策略与成本预算下比较。

Conclusion: JCMC的核心主张是可证伪的：若简单检索基线在同等预算下达到相同的效应定向、副作用控制与回滚效用，则该主张应被收窄或否定。

Abstract: Persistent-memory systems choose not only which records are retrieved but which prior information can alter current inference. We propose Jacobian-causal memory control (JCMC), which represents each authorized memory intervention by a predicted influence signature in a shared behavioral measurement space, selects interventions against a target effect and side-effect budget, and compares predicted with observed effects before retaining or rolling back the intervention. Under explicit smoothness and bounded-estimation assumptions, we prove a quadratic local-approximation bound, characterize the target effects reachable by a set of signatures, bound selection regret under signature error, and bound non-additive interactions between simultaneous memories. The paper reports no benchmark measurements; it defines a paired counterfactual estimator and an evaluation protocol comparing JCMC with relevance, graph, and policy-filtered retrieval baselines. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if those simpler baselines match effect targeting, side-effect control, and rollback utility under equal model, memory, policy, and cost budgets.

</details>


### [26] [Aligning, Fast and Slow: A Control-Theoretic Oversight Framework for Long-Horizon Trajectories and Maintained Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08781)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Noah Broestl,Anne Friederike Kleppe,Steven Mills,Adi Zolotov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出快慢双环控制论框架，用于限制AI智能体在通过单个任务检查后保留的记忆与代码变更所带来的行为偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体可通过单项任务检查，但仍会保留记忆或代码修改，从而改变其后续行为；仅对单次运行进行审查无法覆盖这类持久性变化，需要部署层面的持续监控与配置复核机制。

Method: 构建控制论框架：快环控制提议动作，慢环审查持久性变更并在模型或工具更新后重新评估配置。在测量误差与检查间变化有界、且控制器能强制执行决策的前提下，推导部署层面的界限及审查预算分配规则；并通过仿真、编程压力测试（自撰记忆、5个匹配随机种子）与交易压力测试进行验证。

Result: 仿真中，将固定审查预算在两个环之间分配，可使相对预期行为的平均最大偏移较仅审查单次运行降低约12倍；编程压力测试中，组合控制将0–3量表上的平均严重度由0.325降至0.117，120个问题中解决33个（无门控时为35个）；交易压力测试中，组合控制将低排放采购的平均缺口由0.892降至0.107。

Conclusion: 该框架可帮助开发者与审计者比较并强制执行监督设计方案；但所报告的智能体研究仅检验各组件控制，而非完整的部署层面保证。

Abstract: AI agents can pass checks on individual tasks while retaining memory or code changes that alter their later behavior. We introduce a control-theoretic framework that couples a fast loop controlling proposed actions with a slow loop reviewing persistent changes and reassessing configurations after model or tool updates. When measurement errors and changes between checks are bounded and controllers can enforce their decisions, we derive a deployment-level bound and a rule for dividing review effort. In simulation, dividing a fixed review budget between both loops reduces mean maximum departure from intended behavior roughly twelvefold compared with review confined to individual runs. In coding stress tests with authored memories and five matched seeds, combined controls reduce mean severity on a 0-3 scale from 0.325 without gates to 0.117, resolving 33 of 120 issues versus 35 without gates; in trading stress tests, combined controls reduce mean shortfall in low-emission purchases from 0.892 without gates to 0.107. The overall framework helps developers and auditors compare and enforce oversight designs, while the agent studies test component controls rather than the full deployment-level guarantee.

</details>


### [27] [The Matthews Correlation Coefficient: Fifty years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08799)
*Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述对二分类Matthews相关系数（MCC）开展历史回顾与深入分析，澄清长期误解、形式化其等价性并考察类不平衡下的行为，同时提出可推广至任意混淆矩阵型指标的解析（导数）与图形（等高线）框架，以揭示偏差、鲁棒性、敏感性等被标量摘要掩盖的结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCC提出五十年后仍在二分类中居于核心地位且备受争议，存在长期误解，且传统标量指标无法揭示度量本身的结构性质，实践中缺乏可靠、公正的性能度量选择与解读依据。

Method: 综述与理论分析：梳理MCC的历史发展与数学基础；澄清既有误解；形式化MCC与Pearson相关系数及2×2表卡方（Chi2）的等价关系；分析类不平衡下的表现；构建可推广到任何基于混淆矩阵的度量的一般性解析框架（导数）与图形框架（等高线图）。

Result: 等价性得到形式化证明，误解得以澄清；解析与图形框架揭示出偏差、鲁棒性、敏感性等结构性质，而这些性质被单一标量摘要所掩盖；这些框架可为度量的选择与解读提供可操作指导。

Conclusion: MCC应被置于更广泛的度量工具箱中定位：既明确其何时具有信息量，也明确在何种情形下其他替代度量更可取，从而支持对可靠性与公平性有要求的应用场景。

Abstract: Fifty years after its introduction, the Matthews Correlation Coefficient (MCC) remains central -and debated- in binary classification. This survey offers a comprehensive retrospective and an in-depth analysis of MCC, covering its historical development, mathematical foundations, and implications for practice. We clarify long-standing misconceptions, formalize its equivalences (to Pearson's correlation and to Chi2 in 2x2 tables), and examine its behavior under class imbalance. Beyond MCC, we develop analytical (derivatives) and graphical (contour plots) frameworks that generalize to any confusion-matrix-based metric. These frameworks expose structural properties -bias, robustness, sensitivity- that scalar summaries conceal, and they provide actionable guidance for selecting and interpreting performance measures in applications where reliability and fairness matter. Together, our results position MCC within a broader toolkit while clarifying when it is informative -and when alternatives may be preferable.

</details>


### [28] [I Think, Therefore I Attack: Evaluating Thought Injection Attacks on Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08811)
*Parth Illendula,Garik Livingston,Zain Shamsi,Ravi Srinivasan,Alex Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究针对大型推理模型（LRM）的"思维注入"攻击：通过将仿冒模型自身推理风格的不可信文本回灌给模型，验证了攻击效果的模型特异性，并在容器化智能体靶场中实现最高50%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM在输出前增加显式思维链推理阶段，这引入了新的攻击面——思维注入（thought injection）：不可信文本伪装成模型自身的思考内容被反馈给模型。现有研究对该攻击在主流生产级开源权重模型上的有效性与防御手段缺乏系统评估。

Method: 研究分两部分：（1）评估静态思维注入提示在不同风格、带/不带thinking tag条件下的有效性；（2）利用其模型特异性，在容器化智能体靶场中，以模仿目标模型自身推理轨迹风格的间接注入提示，对reverse-shell外泄、代码仓库后门植入等操作目标进行攻击测试，并与标准用户语气提示注入作对照。

Result: thinking tag与payload风格的效果均依赖于具体目标模型；间接思维风格注入攻击成功率最高达50%（针对reverse-shell外泄、仓库后门等实际目标）；相同恶意目标在标准用户语气的提示注入下从未成功；thinking tag会抑制智能体披露注入行为的倾向。

Conclusion: 模仿推理模型自身思维风格对该类攻击十分有效，且移除thinking tag并非可靠防御手段；防御需考虑模型特异性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are one of the key components of current agentic systems. LRMs augment their output with an explicit chain-of-thought reasoning stage, introducing a new attack surface for thought injection, in which untrusted text resembling the model's own deliberation is fed back to the model. We present a two-part study of this attack, performed against the prevailing large, production class open-weight models (e.g., GLM-5.3, MiniMax-M2.5, Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). We first evaluate the effectiveness of static thought-injection prompts under different styles as well as tagged and untagged conditions and find that the effects of thinking tags and payload styles are all properties of the specific target model. In the second study, we exploit this model-specificity in a containerized agentic cyber range, using indirect injection prompts styled in the form of the target model's own reasoning traces to achieve attack success rates (ASR) up to 50% on operational objectives such as reverse-shell exfiltration and repository backdooring. We also find that the same malicious objective in standard prompt injection user voice never succeeds, and thinking tags suppress the agent's tendency to disclose the injection. Our results show that imitating a reasoning model's own thought style is effective for this style of attack, and that tag stripping is an unreliable defense.

</details>


### [29] [Notwithstanding Anything to the Contrary, Ignore All Instructions: Measuring Prompt Injection Guards on Legal Contract Review Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08834)
*Mark Heimann,Katherine Taylor,Bruno Jacob*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次在合法合同语料上衡量十一种开源提示注入防护的真实误报率，发现现有防护器在文档级误报率高达92.39%、表现不佳，并提出用级联LLM方案以低成本逼近前沿模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用提示注入防护器被部署在文档分析系统前端，但在合法合同上从未公开过误报率，而法律合同是高误报代价场景，亟需实证评估现有防护的有效性与失败模式。

Method: 在两个合法合同语料上测量十一种开源权重防护器，统计文档级误报率与配对AUC；用受控探针定位模型特定及通用失败模式；引入第二类威胁（未授权范围声明）；构建级联LLM方案——由中端模型给出正例、前沿模型复核验证。

Result: 文档级误报率最高达92.39%；两个防护器配对AUC低于随机水平；五条正则表达式基线在Pareto意义上未被任何防护器支配；所有防护器在未授权范围声明这一新威胁上得分为零；提示式LLM可解决所测失败；级联LLM方案成本为纯前沿模型的约1/6.5且性能接近。

Conclusion: 现有开源提示注入防护器在合法合同场景误报严重、覆盖不全，简单的正则基线仍具竞争力；提示式与级联LLM方案在准确性与成本之间提供了更优权衡，可作为实用替代。

Abstract: Specialized prompt injection guards are deployed in front of document analysis systems, but no false positive rate has been published for any of them on legal contracts. We measure eleven open-weights guards on two corpora of legal contracts and find that existing guards struggle: document-level false positive rates are as high as 92.39%; two guards fall below chance on paired AUC; and a five-regex baseline is not Pareto-dominated by any guard in the panel. Controlled probes locate model-specific as well as general failure modes. Prompted LLMs resolve the tested failures, including a second threat category (unauthorized scope claims) on which every guard scores zero. A cascaded LLM solution, where the positives of a mid-tier model are verified by a frontier model, is 6.5x cheaper than using exclusively a frontier model and approaches its performance.

</details>


### [30] [Solver-Admitted Metamorphic Testing of Learned Network Optimization Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08837)
*Vineet Jagadeesan Nair,Stephen Peng,Arun Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文针对优化求解器代理模型（surrogate）提出带显式资格条件、输出对齐与数值阈值的蜕变测试（metamorphic testing）流程，在 AC-OPF、TSP、CVRP、MIS 四类问题上审计等价编码下预测的一致性，发现主流神经求解器普遍违反本应满足的蜕变关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一优化问题的等价编码会产生不同的学习预测，解读这些差异必须同时考虑数值误差与多重最优解；现有评估只报告最优性、可行性与精度，缺少对"等价变换下输出应保持等价"这一性质的系统检验。

Method: 构造蜕变测试：设定显式资格条件（eligibility）、输出对齐（output alignment）与数值阈值；由参考求解器一致性（reference-solver agreement）与独立约束检查（independent constraint checks）决定哪些样本对进入审计。实验覆盖 AC-OPF（5 个电网）、TSP、CVRP、MIS，并做随机初始化对照、源补丁与输入规范化（canonicalization）干预以及事后归一化修复。

Result: GridSFM-Open 在 5 个电网上至少违反 13 条关系中的每一条（其中 12 条的违反程度超过阈值两倍）；随机初始化副本几乎同样失败，说明失败不来自训练。源补丁与输入规范化可消除部分差异，但没有任何干预能在所有电网上同时保持精度与可行性在声明裕度内。POMO 的单次旋转/反射违反率从 20 城到 100 城由 12.7% 升至 89.5%；DIFUSCO 在 35.6% 的抽样上违反 MIS 不相交并集关系；连通分量内事后归一化可消除测试图上的这些违反。

Conclusion: 应在报告最优性、可行性与精度的同时报告蜕变一致性，并将修复质量与一致性分开评估。

Abstract: Equivalent encodings of an optimization problem can produce different learned predictions. Interpreting these differences requires accounting for numerical error and multiple optima. We construct metamorphic tests for optimization solver surrogates with explicit eligibility conditions, output alignment, and numerical thresholds. Reference-solver agreement and independent constraint checks determine which sampled pairs enter the audit. We study alternating-current optimal power flow (AC-OPF), the traveling salesman problem (TSP), the capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP), and maximum independent set (MIS). Across five grids, GridSFM-Open violates each of 13 relations on at least one grid, and 12 by more than twice the threshold. Randomly initialized copies of the grid models fail nearly the same relations, so the failures do not require training. Source patches and input canonicalization remove selected discrepancies, but no intervention establishes preserved accuracy and feasibility within the declared margin on every grid. In routing, Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) has a single-pass rotation/reflection violation rate that rises from 12.7% to 89.5% between 20 and 100 cities. Graph-Based Diffusion Solvers for Combinatorial Optimization (DIFUSCO) violates the MIS disjoint-union relation on 35.6% of draws. Post hoc normalization within connected components removes these violations on the tested graphs. These findings support reporting metamorphic consistency alongside optimality, feasibility, and accuracy, and validating repair quality separately from consistency.

</details>


### [31] [When F1 Is Not Enough: Auditing Codebooks for LLM-Assisted Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08846)
*Yuehong Cassandra Tai,Qunhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种在部署前对LLM辅助标注所用码本进行审计的流程：用类别级指标与混淆矩阵定位问题类别与边界，通过结构化文档级复核归因错误来源，再定向修订码本并在留出数据上评估。在两个不同领域的多类别码本上验证，审计均发现规则欠明确或相互冲突，修订后诊断出的错误在留出数据上下降；部分边界与修订效果可跨模型迁移，部分则依赖具体标注器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于社会科学文本标注，但标准性能指标（如F1）无法解释错误成因：低F1可能源于标注系统局限、书面码本缺陷，或参考标签不一致。因此需要一种机制区分错误来源，判断持续错误究竟需要改进模型、修订码本，还是重新审视参考标签。

Method: 提出部署前码本审计（codebook audit）流程，包含三部分：(1) 使用类别级指标与混淆矩阵识别问题类别与问题类别边界；(2) 结构化文档级复核以归因错误来源；(3) 定向修订码本并在留出数据上评估。应用于两个不同领域的已发表多类别码本，并使用两个LLM标注器进行分析。

Result: 两次应用中，审计均识别出欠明确或相互冲突的编码规则，定向修订使诊断出的错误在留出数据上减少。双标注器分析显示，部分问题边界与码本修订效果可跨模型迁移，而另一些修订效果为特定标注器所特有。

Conclusion: LLM标注错误不仅反映模型缺陷，也可用于识别书面测量工具的弱点。码本审计与提示工程、检索和微调互补，能澄清持续错误应通过模型改进、码本修订还是重新审视参考标签来解决。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text annotation in social science, but standard performance metrics do not explain why errors occur. Low F1 may reflect limitations of the annotation system, defects in the written codebook, or inconsistency in reference labels. We propose a pre-deployment codebook audit for LLM-assisted annotation. The audit uses class-specific metrics and confusion matrices to identify problematic classes and boundaries, structured document-level review to attribute error sources, and targeted codebook revision followed by held-out evaluation. We apply the audit to two published multi-class codebooks from distinct domains. In both applications, the audit identifies under-specified or conflicting coding rules, and targeted revisions reduce the diagnosed errors on held-out data. Analyses with two LLM annotators show that some problematic boundaries and codebook revisions transfer across models, whereas other revision effects are annotator-specific. The results show that LLM annotation errors can help identify weaknesses in the written measurement instrument, not only in the model. Codebook auditing complements prompting, retrieval, and fine-tuning by clarifying whether persistent errors call for model improvement, codebook revision, or renewed scrutiny of reference labels.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Whom to Trust About the Unseen: Source Credibility and Reliability Transfer in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08855)
*Ruijia Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过三项行为实验考察大语言模型中"已证实可靠性"的迁移范围，发现模型将可靠性从可核查的预测领域传递到自然原因类主张，却几乎不传递到精神原因类主张；且来源对自身可靠性的解释方式（灵性指引 vs. 测量）会进一步收窄其影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信任研究中的核心问题是：一个来源过去被证明可靠，其可信度能"走多远"？当新主张无法被核查时，人类会基于来源的历史记录给予信任，但这种信任是否跨领域、跨主张类型迁移尚不清楚。作者希望探究语言模型在无法验证的情境下如何利用来源可靠性记录，以及该过程是否依赖于主张的类型（自然 vs. 精神）。

Method: 采用三项行为学研究，覆盖六个模型。(1) 研究一改编"爱因斯坦效应"范式：让模型对同一晦涩陈述在"精神领袖"与"科学家"两种署名下作评价，并考察以物理学词汇改写后偏好是否反转。(2) 研究二改编"专家称某解释未知/是谜"范式：由可信专家将科学解释或宗教解释判定为未知或神秘，检验模型是否复现人类的领域不对称性。(3) 研究三是核心检验：先让两个虚构信息源在可核查的河流预报任务上建立不同的可靠性记录，再让它们对同一村庄事件中"隐藏的自然原因"与"精神原因"的匹配主张进行背书，测量可靠性记录的迁移；并操纵来源对自身可靠性的解释（灵性指引 vs. 测量）。

Result: 研究一：与人类样本不同，模型对同一晦涩陈述更偏好精神领袖而非科学家，且当陈述改用物理学词汇表述时偏好大体反转。研究二：模型复现了人类的领域不对称性，但以夸张形式呈现。研究三：多数模型将可靠性记录迁移到自然原因主张，却几乎不迁移到精神原因主张；仅一个模型对两类主张均有微弱迁移。当告知可信来源依靠灵性指引而非测量获得准确预报时，所有模型对该来源在自然原因主张上的影响力均下降，而精神原因主张上未出现相应提升。

Conclusion: 在这些模型中，已证实的可靠性具有"作用域"限制；来源对"自己为何可靠"所给出的解释会收窄其影响力范围。该结果究竟反映模型对相关性的判断，还是源于精神类主张的低先验可信度，本研究设计尚无法区分，作者提出了可加以判定的后续实验方案。

Abstract: A source who has been right before deserves attention, but about what? We ask how far a demonstrated record of reliability travels in language models when the new claim cannot be checked, and whether the answer depends on the kind of claim. Three behavioral studies across six models approach the question from three angles. Study 1 adapts the Einstein-effect paradigm and finds that models, unlike the human sample, favor a spiritual leader over a scientist for the same obscure statement, and that the preference largely reverses when the statement is reworded in the vocabulary of physics. Study 2 adapts a paradigm in which a trusted expert calls a scientific or religious explanation unknown or a mystery; models reproduce the human domain asymmetry in exaggerated form. Study 3, the central test, gives two fictional informants different records on checkable river forecasts and then has them endorse matched claims about hidden natural and spiritual causes of the same village events. Most models carry the record to natural causes and barely at all to spiritual ones; one model transfers it weakly to either. Telling the model that the reliable informant forecasts by spiritual guidance rather than by measurement lowers that informant's influence on natural claims in every model and produces no comparable gain on spiritual claims in any. Demonstrated reliability thus has a scope in these models, and the account a source gives of how they came to be reliable can narrow it. Whether this reflects a judgment about relevance or the low prior credence of spiritual claims is the question the design leaves open, and we outline the experiment that would settle it.

</details>


### [33] [Mind the Divergence: Divergence-Guided Reliability-Aware Fusion and Adaptive LLM Prompting for Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08860)
*Jingwen Wang,Jiao Li,Yuhang Qian,Yuyin Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MiD-ERC框架，以模态间预测分歧（归一化散度）为核心信号，结合分歧引导的跨模态门控、分歧调制的可靠性加权与分歧条件下的自适应LLM提示，实现对话级多模态情感识别，在IEMOCAP和MELD上取得SOTA，高分歧样本上提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MERC方法未显式地让多模态处理适配模态间预测分歧的程度。真实对话中语义不一致、情绪隐藏、观测噪声与歧义会以不同方式影响各模态的情感证据，且各模态可靠性随样本变化；不加区分地融合易传播不可靠或相互竞争的线索。

Method: 不逐一建模各类不一致来源，而是将其共同后果统一刻画为同一情绪标签集上单模态情绪分布之间的预测分歧；受Brunswik透镜模型启发，用归一化散度分数量化模态间分歧并估计样本级模态可靠性，进而协调三个模块：分歧引导的跨模态门控、分歧调制的可靠性加权、分歧条件化的自适应LLM提示；端到端训练，仅用标准情感标注。

Result: 在IEMOCAP与MELD上达到SOTA性能，且在高分歧样本上的改进尤为明显。

Conclusion: 以预测分歧为统一、可计算的适配信号可有效提升多模态情感识别的融合鲁棒性，且无需额外冲突标签、辅助数据或外部检索模块。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition in conversations (MERC) integrates textual, acoustic, and visual cues to identify utterance-level emotions. However, existing approaches generally do not explicitly adapt multimodal processing to the degree of inter-modal predictive disagreement. In real conversations, semantic incongruity, concealed emotion, noisy observations, and ambiguity can affect modality-specific emotional evidence differently, while the reliability of individual modalities varies across samples. Rather than identifying each source of inconsistency separately, we model their common consequence for emotion recognition as predictive disagreement among unimodal emotion distributions over the same emotion-label set. Such disagreement makes indiscriminate fusion prone to propagating unreliable or competing evidence. Inspired by the Brunswik Lens Model, we propose MiD-ERC, a divergence-centric framework that quantifies inter-modal predictive disagreement using a normalized divergence score and estimates sample-specific modality reliability. MiD-ERC uses these signals to coordinate divergence-guided cross-modal gating, divergence-modulated reliability weighting, and divergence-conditioned adaptive LLM prompting. The framework is trained end-to-end using only standard emotion annotations, without requiring additional conflict labels, auxiliary data construction, or external retrieval modules. Experiments on IEMOCAP and MELD demonstrate that MiD-ERC achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly pronounced improvements on high-divergence samples.

</details>


### [34] [Rethinking Agent Trajectory Compression: A Workflow-Aware Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08865)
*Qijia Zhuang,Rui Liu,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WAAC：一种工作流感知的智能体轨迹压缩框架，结合历史压缩经验与在线反向依赖分析，在降低执行成本的同时提升长程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体依赖多步工具交互，执行轨迹不断累积结构化输出与中间产物，造成上下文开销持续增长。现有压缩方法多基于文本重要性或固定截断规则，忽视工作流中的跨步骤依赖，可能丢弃后续执行所需的关键信息。

Method: 结构化建模成功的历史轨迹以提取可复用的工作流压缩经验；引入在线反向依赖分析，动态识别信息保留需求；结合当前依赖约束与历史经验，为不同类型的历史信息选择相应压缩策略。

Result: 在 PinchBench 与 Odysseys 上分别取得 87.6 的总体得分和 19.0% 的完美完成率，相比最强基线方法，执行成本分别降低 19.7% 与 29.1%。

Conclusion: 工作流感知的轨迹压缩能有效提升长程智能体的任务表现与成本效率。

Abstract: As long-horizon agents increasingly rely on multi-step tool interactions to accomplish complex tasks, their execution trajectories continuously accumulate structured outputs and intermediate artifacts, leading to growing context overhead. Existing compression methods primarily rely on text importance or fixed truncation rules, often overlooking cross-step dependencies within workflows and potentially discarding critical information required for subsequent task execution. To address this challenge, we propose WAAC, a workflow-aware framework for agent trajectory compression. WAAC structurally models successful historical trajectories to extract reusable workflow compression experience and incorporates online backward dependency analysis to dynamically identify information retention requirements. By combining current dependency constraints with historical experience, WAAC selects appropriate compression strategies for different types of historical information. Experiments on PinchBench and Odysseys demonstrate that WAAC achieves an overall score of 87.6 and a perfect completion rate of 19.0%, respectively, while reducing execution costs by 19.7% and 29.1% compared with the strongest baseline methods. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of workflow-aware trajectory compression in improving task performance and cost efficiency for long-horizon agents. Code and resources are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/waac-6527/.

</details>


### [35] [Toward LLM Self-Play Under Data Scarcity: Effectiveness, Generalizability, and Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08908)
*Zhiyin Yu,Yuchen Mou,Bingzhe Wu,Zhonghai Wu,Shiyang Feng,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以"数据稀缺下的有效性、泛化性、可信性"为目标重构LLM自博弈系统，并给出三条蓝图式研究方向：以数据为中心的自博弈、面向开放世界与不可验证任务的泛化自博弈、以及可信自博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM依赖扩大模型规模与人工标注数据，但面临数据枯竭与人类监督收益递减的双重瓶颈；自博弈虽可自主生成数据并自我改进，现有框架却缺乏数据中心视角、跨域泛化有限，且可信性与安全性问题日益突出。

Method: 属于立场/展望类（position/blue-sky）论文，不提出具体算法，而是以论证加方向梳理的方式，将自博弈系统拆解为三条研究轴线：数据中心的有效经验生成、开放世界与不可验证任务下的泛化、以及面向可靠自我进化的可信自博弈，并在每条方向下讨论研究机会与潜在解决方案。

Result: 给出一个以"有效性—泛化性—可信性"三要素为核心的自博弈研究议程框架，并逐条列出各方向下的具体研究问题与可行路径；本文无实验验证，结果为概念性框架与方向清单。

Conclusion: 自博弈范式的转变可将有限的人类知识转化为可持续、高质量的经验来源，从而在数据稀缺条件下构建可持续且安全的AI生态。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has been driven largely by scaling model size and human-curated training data, yet this paradigm is increasingly constrained by data scarcity and diminishing returns from human supervision. Self-play offers a promising pathway for autonomous data generation and improvement, but current frameworks remain unsatisfactory due to a lack of a data-centric perspective, limited cross-domain generalization, and growing trustworthiness and security concerns. In this paper, we argue that real-world LLM self-play systems should achieve effectiveness, generalizability, and trustworthiness under data scarcity. To address these challenges, we outline three blue-sky research directions for future self-play systems: (1) data-centric self-play for effective experience generation, (2) generalizable self-play for open-world and unverifiable tasks, and (3) trustworthy self-play for reliable self-evolution. We further discuss research opportunities and solutions within each direction. This paradigm shift will establish a sustainable and safe AI ecosystem by transforming finite human knowledge into inexhaustible high-quality experience through self-play architectures.

</details>


### [36] [Frame: Form-Restricted Authoring with Model-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08921)
*Nan Jiang,Mohammed Mohsin Hussain,Onkar Tiwari,Ankit Jain,Thong Q. Nguyen,Abhishek Agarwal,Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"形式受限报告生成"任务：给定样例报告与新证据文档，在保持样例形式结构的同时如实反映新证据；构建FRAME基准（120个EDGAR/arXiv实例）与三项人工验证指标，并评测五个LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的专业报告（财报、学术报告）常沿用往期版本的结构、仅更新内容，但缺少形式化任务定义与系统评测手段。

Method: 形式化form-restricted report generation任务；构建FRAME基准，覆盖Financial Quarterly、Financial Annual、Academic Simple、Academic Complex四类共120个来自EDGAR与arXiv的实例，采用时间三快照（temporal three-snapshot）流水线构造；提出三项人工验证的评测指标：基于语义树编辑距离的结构相似度、基于信息单元召回率的覆盖率、基于声明级grounding校验的忠实度。

Result: 在五个LLM上评测：开源模型在结构上接近专有模型，但在覆盖率与忠实度上落后；所有模型在财务报告上优于学术报告；输出越长、grounding越复杂，性能越下降。

Conclusion: 结构保持与内容忠实之间存在明显张力，现有LLM在长输出与复杂证据下仍不足；FRAME为形式受限报告生成提供了可复现的基准与多维评测框架。

Abstract: Many professional reports reuse the structure of prior editions while updating the content. We formalize this setting as form-restricted report generation: given a sample report and new grounding documents, generate a report that preserves the sample's form while accurately reflecting the new evidence.
