*Bo Qu,Zhurong Wang,Frank Zahradnik,Ming Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"模型外挂"框架：不改动已部署的GBDT欺诈检测模型，让多个LLM生成锚定在SHAP低权重特征上的规则，经经验支持筛选与校准后形成可加、可逆的log-lift叠加层，在生产阈值处提升召回/精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业表格欺诈分类中梯度提升树已是最优，但存在盲区；而重训已部署模型并不总是安全（选择偏差数据下继续训练会导致性能回退），因此需要一个不触碰原模型、可回退的补丁机制。

Method: 以SHAP识别模型低估的特征作为锚点，由LLM提出规则构成带噪规则池；对规则池做经验支持度筛查并校准，得到加性、可逆的log-lift叠加层，在生产cut-off处生效。为抑制单LLM高方差（且跨数据集收益反转），对三个异构骨干做等权logit平均集成。

Result: 真实电商欺诈检测器（选择偏差数据）上，精调后的继续训练仍回退，而叠加层在30–57%精确率、23–63%召回的生产阈值区间内平均取得+2.26% ΔR@P与+1.60% ΔP@R；在七个设计偏差偏移变体的公开基准上，全部变体的ΔP@R均提升，ΔR@P基本持平。

Conclusion: 该叠加式补丁在不修改、不需重训的前提下稳定优于继续训练，且每条修正都是可读谓词、可逆可审计，兼顾性能与部署安全性。

Abstract: In industrial tabular fraud classification, gradient-boosted trees remain state-of-the-art, but even a well-trained model has blind spots, and retraining the deployed model is not always safe. We propose a framework that patches such a model without modifying it: LLMs propose rules anchored on features the model underweights by SHAP, and the resulting noisy pool is screened for empirical support and calibrated into an additive, reversible log-lift overlay that runs at the production cut-off. Single-LLM benefit is high-variance and inverts between datasets, so we pool three diverse backbones by equal-weight logit averaging, trading peak gain for a bounded worst case. On a real-world e-commerce fraud detector trained on selection-biased data, continued training regresses even when carefully tuned, whereas the overlay gains on average +2.26% recall at equal precision ($Δ$R@P) and +1.60% precision at equal recall ($Δ$P@R) over production cut-offs spanning 30-57% precision and 23-63% recall. On a public benchmark of seven designed bias-shift variants it raises $Δ$P@R on every variant and keeps $Δ$R@P near neutral. Each amendment is a readable predicate naming the fraud pattern it covers.

</details>


### [141] [On Differentially Private Mechanisms for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08737)
*Bardiya Aryanfard,Monika Henzinger,Farhood Rostamkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContinualAdaSSP，首个在持续差分隐私下处理无约束最小二乘线性回归的机制，并首次给出盒约束线性回归的DP下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量设定下DP线性回归研究充分，但持续观测设定（每步需输出参数估计）研究不足，既有工作仅覆盖约束线性回归且更新时间关于n为多项式。

Method: 将AdaSSP类思想推广到持续观测：以O(d^2 log n)空间、O(d^2 log n + d^ω)更新时间维护充分统计量的隐私估计并逐步输出；下界方面通过从fingerprinting code问题（Peter et al., 2024）归约。

Result: 机制次优间隙为√d·polylog(n)，空间O(d^2 log n)、更新时间O(d^2 log n + d^ω)；盒约束下界对(1,O(1/n))-DP为min(n, √d/log^{1.5}d)，对ε-DP为min(n, d/ε)；真实数据实验优于逐步重跑批量算法等方案。

Conclusion: 给出持续DP无约束线性回归的首个高效算法及约束情形首批下界，实验验证其精度优势。

Abstract: We study the classic least-squares linear regression problem under differential privacy (DP) in the batch and the continual observation setting. In the latter setting, the mechanism receives a stream of $n$ data points $(x_i,y_i)$ ($x_i\in\mathbb{R}^d$ and $y_i\in\mathbb{R}$) and must output an estimate of the optimal parameters at each time step $i$. Although differentially private linear regression has been extensively studied in the batch setting, the problem remains underexplored for the continual setting, with prior work being restricted to constrained linear regression and update time polynomial in $n$.
  In this work, we introduce ContinualAdaSSP, the first mechanism for unconstrained linear regression under continual differential privacy. Our mechanism's suboptimality gap is proportional to $\sqrt{d}\operatorname{polylog}(n)$. It requires $O(d^2\log{n})$ space and $O(d^2\log{n}+d^ω)$ update time, where $d$ is the dimension of the input space.
  We also study fundamental limits of differentially private linear regression by showing the first lower bounds for the box-constrained least-squares linear regression problem. Our lower bounds are proportional to $\min(n,\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\log^{1.5}d})$ for $(1,O(\frac{1}{n}))$-DP, and $\min(n,\frac{d}ε)$ for $ε$-DP, and are derived using a reduction from a fingerprinting code problem from [Peter et al., 2024], providing new insights into the inherent hardness of this canonical task.
  Our experimental evaluation on real-world datasets shows that the accuracy of our continual observation mechanism clearly outperforms any other known differentially private mechanism for this problem such as rerunning the batch algorithm at each time step.

</details>


### [142] [Random Symbolic Boosting: A Geometric Approach to Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08753)
*José R. Ortegaa,José Manuel Muñoz Contreras,Leonardo Trujillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机符号提升（RSB）：通过对随机生成的语法树种群做提升，构建显式可加符号模型，避免代际演化；结合向量空间视角与自适应几何早停，在可解释性、精度与训练时间上取得Pareto最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号模型可解释性好，但依赖代际演化的构建方式计算开销大，需要一种无需演化即可高效构建显式符号模型的方法。

Method: 基于两个观察：高维空间可由足够多线性无关向量张成，语法树的语义可直接当作向量操作。据此对随机生成的语法树种群进行boosting集成，构建显式可加符号模型；理论上证明在OLS下boosting对回归与分类均单调降低训练误差；并提出基于预测向量与目标向量夹角的自适应几何早停机制，与问题尺度无关、无需按任务调参。

Result: 在符号回归标准基准上，方法位于预测精度与训练时间的Pareto最优点，在真实数据集上排名第一；在含200个问题的大规模表格分类基准上取得有竞争力结果。

Conclusion: RSB表明以随机语法树池+boosting替代代际演化可显著降低符号建模成本，兼具理论收敛保证与免调参早停，在回归与分类任务上均具竞争力。

Abstract: Symbolic models offer interpretability, but their construction through generational evolution can be computationally expensive. This article presents Random Symbolic Boosting (RSB), a method that constructs explicit additive symbolic models via boosting over populations of randomly generated syntax trees, eliminating the need for generational evolution. The idea combines two observations: high-dimensional spaces can be spanned by enough linearly independent vectors, and the semantics of syntax trees can be directly manipulated as such vectors. It is shown that, for Ordinary Least Squares, the boosting process guarantees a monotonic reduction of the training error for both regression and classification. Furthermore, an adaptive geometric early stopping mechanism is introduced. It is based on the angle between the prediction and target vectors, is independent of the problems scale, and requires no adjustments across tasks. Evaluated on a standard benchmark for symbolic regression, the method is located at the Pareto-optimal point between predictive accuracy and training time, ranking first on the real-world datasets. Additionally, it was tested on a large tabular classification benchmark with 200 problems, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [143] [MEDALLION: Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08767)
*Vicky Jung,Claire M. He,Irene Chang,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEDALLION：通过知识蒸馏与神经特征归因解释任意降维嵌入（如t-SNE、UMAP），将黑盒嵌入蒸馏为可解释的神经编码器并用现有归因方法定位贡献特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维广泛用于高维数据可视化与科学发现，但对t-SNE、UMAP等黑盒方法而言，判断哪些原始特征贡献了低维表示仍很困难。

Method: 提出MEDALLION框架：先用神经编码器蒸馏给定嵌入（复现其低维表示），再对该编码器应用多种现有神经特征归因方法，从而把归因结果回传到原始特征空间。

Result: 在MNIST实验及单细胞转录组学应用中，MEDALLION给出有信息量的特征归因，为理解底层嵌入提供额外洞见，并与现有可解释性方法进行了性能比较。

Conclusion: 以蒸馏+归因替代直接解释黑盒降维，是一种通用且有效的嵌入可解释性路径。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is widely used to visualize high-dimensional data and guide scientific discovery, yet interpreting which original features contribute to low-dimensional representations remains challenging, particularly for black-box methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. We introduce \textbf{MEDALLION} (Manifold Embedding Distillation and Attributions for Low-dimensional Latent InterpretatiON), a novel framework for interpreting low-dimensional embeddings through knowledge distillation and neural feature attribution. MEDALLION distills any given embedding with a neural encoder which we explain via a broad range of existing neural feature attribution methods. Through experiments on MNIST and applications in single-cell transcriptomics, we show MEDALLION provides informative feature attributions that cast additional insights into the underlying embedding and compare its performance with existing interpretability approaches.

</details>


### [144] [On Lipschitz Explosion in Deep Neural Networks with Normalization: Consequences for Optimization and Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08775)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Reyhaneh Hosseinpourkhoshkbari,Hadi Daneshmand,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：带有归一化层（如 BatchNorm）的深层网络在逐层谱范数受控（‖Wℓ‖₂≤1）下，输入 Lipschitz 常数与参数 Lipschitz 常数仍可随深度（即参数维度）指数增长，即使激活函数为线性；由此使基于 Lipschitz 的优化保证、泛化界与鲁棒性证书失效，并提出"秩创造型扰动"危害更大的机制，MNIST/Fashion-MNIST/CIFAR-10 实验予以支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lipschitz 常数是优化保证、泛化界与鲁棒性证书中的基础稳定性度量，而深层网络天然存在输入 Lipschitz 性与参数 Lipschitz 性两种概念。实践者常用逐层范数控制（如谱归一化、‖Wℓ‖₂≤1）与归一化层来"稳定"训练，但局部逐层有界是否能推出整体稳定尚不明确。作者希望揭示归一化层这一被广泛用于稳定训练的机制，可能制造出仅凭逐层范数边界无法察觉的严重最坏情形不稳定性，并解释对抗脆弱性的结构性来源。

Method: 理论构造与证明：在含归一化层（以 BatchNorm 为典型）的深网中，即使在强逐层范数约束 ‖Wℓ‖₂≤1 下，且激活取线性，仍可给出输入/参数 Lipschitz 常数随深度指数增长的构造性证明；分析其对 Lipschitz 依赖的非光滑优化保证、泛化界与鲁棒性证书的影响，并推导出一个基于秩分离（rank-separation）的对抗脆弱性机制。实证部分：在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10 上对比"引入新奇异方向（秩创造）"的扰动与"保持原奇异子空间内"的同强度扰动对精度、置信度与 margin 的影响。

Result: (1) 存在归一化深网的输入 Lipschitz 常数与参数 Lipschitz 常数均随深度/参数维度指数增长，且与逐层谱范数有界相容；(2) 参数 Lipschitz 爆炸使依赖 Lipschitz 的非光滑优化保证退化为维度指数级界，输入 Lipschitz 爆炸使基于最坏情形 Lipschitz 的泛化与鲁棒性证书变为空泛；(3) 理论预测得到实验验证：秩创造型扰动相比同噪声水平、同子空间扰动，造成精度、置信度与 margin 更显著下降。

Conclusion: 逐层范数有界与"看起来稳定"并不蕴含全局稳定性：归一化层可使深层网络在输入与参数两个方向上任一层面出现指数级不稳定性，且这种不稳定性在仅凭逐层范数边界的局部视角下不可见。其后果是 Lipschitz 类优化/泛化/鲁棒性证书失效，并给出对抗脆弱性的秩分离解释；秩创造型扰动是更危险的对抗方向，为鲁棒性评估与正则化设计（如控制谱/秩变化而非仅控逐层范数）提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: Can a network look stable at every layer and still be exponentially unstable as a whole? The Lipschitz constant of a neural network is a basic stability quantity appearing in optimization guarantees, generalization bounds, and robustness certificates. Deep networks naturally admit two such notions, input Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to data perturbations, and parameter Lipschitzness, measuring sensitivity to weight perturbations. We prove that, for deep networks with normalization layers, with batch normalization as the canonical example, both Lipschitz constants can grow exponentially with depth and hence with parameter dimension, even under strong per-layer norm control such as $\|W_\ell\|_2\le 1$, and already for linear activations. Thus, a mechanism widely used to stabilize training can create severe worst-case instabilities that are invisible locally, i.e., from layerwise norm bounds alone. Parameter-Lipschitz explosion turns Lipschitz-dependent nonsmooth optimization guarantees into exponential-in-dimension bounds for deep normalized networks, while input-Lipschitz explosion makes worst-case Lipschitz-based generalization and robustness certificates vacuous. The latter also yields a concrete rank-separation mechanism for adversarial vulnerability. The theory predicts that perturbations introducing new singular directions should be amplified much more strongly than perturbations at the same noise level that remain within the input's original singular subspace. Experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 support this prediction, showing that rank-creating perturbations cause substantially sharper drops in accuracy, confidence, and margins than same-subspace perturbations.

</details>


### [145] [Training Stability under Adaptive Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08787)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Walter Mayor,Michael Dennis,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究UED生成课程下"学生"（学习器）自身的可训练性，识别出参数增长、价值—结果排序退化、表征多样性丧失等学习者侧病理，指出其与停滞和性能退化相关，并通过控制干预证明稳定学习器可提升长期性能与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往UED研究主要关注课程如何构造，而忽视了在自适应课程分布下学习器是否仍保持可训练性；自适应课程会把学习器的优化状态与其未来训练分布耦合起来，因此需要从学习器侧诊断UED的失败模式。

Method: 在多种UED机制、环境复杂度与延长训练时长下进行系统性实证分析，识别反复出现的学习者侧病理；并通过受控干预（在不改变课程规则、不增加环境交互的前提下稳定学习器）检验其因果关系。

Result: 发现参数增长、价值—结果排序退化、表征多样性丧失等病理伴随训练停滞与性能下降；受控干预缓解这些病理后，学习效果改善，长期性能与零样本泛化提升，且无需改变课程规则或增加环境交互。

Conclusion: 将"学习器可训练性"确立为UED的互补设计原则：有效课程不仅取决于课程生成机制，还要求学生在所生成的分布上持续具备学习能力。

Abstract: Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approaches train reinforcement learning (RL) agents on diverse curricula that may evolve with their capabilities. While prior work has largely focused on how these curricula are constructed, we study whether their students remain trainable under the resulting distributions. Across multiple UED mechanisms, environment complexities, and extended training horizons, we identify recurring learner-side pathologies, including parameter growth, degraded value--outcome ordering, and loss of representational diversity, that accompany stagnation and performance degradation. These findings connect limitations in UED to optimization failures previously identified in conventional deep RL, while showing how adaptive curricula couple the learner's optimization state to its future training distribution. Controlled interventions show that mitigating selected pathologies improves learning. Stabilizing the learner improves long-horizon performance and zero-shot generalization without changing the curriculum rule or increasing environment interaction. Our results establish learner trainability as a complementary design principle for UED. Effective curricula require students that remain capable of learning from the distributions they produce.

</details>


### [146] [Not All Latents Are Equal: Importance-Weighted Latent Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08788)
*Biraaj Rout,Jay Revolinsky,Han Liu,Harry Shomer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Importance Reweighting (LIR)：用解码器对潜变量的 Jacobian 衡量各潜维度对图重建的相对影响，并据此对潜维度加权，缓解潜空间图生成中"绝大多数影响集中于少数维度、损失对全潜等权重建"导致的性能落后问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散图扩散（图扩散/流匹配）的计算量随节点数二次增长，难以扩展到大规模图；潜空间图生成维度更低、更具可扩展性，但实际性能落后于离散方法。作者归因于不同潜维度对图重建的贡献分布极不均衡——影响集中在少数维度，而现有损失对整个潜变量等权重建，使模型浪费容量去拟合与下游任务无关的信息。

Method: 以解码器相对于潜变量的 Jacobian 计算单个潜单元对图重建的相对影响，作为该维度的"重要性"；在损失中对各潜维度按重要性重加权（LIR）。方法模型无关，不引入额外参数或超参数。

Result: 在两个不同的自编码器、多个基准数据集上，LIR 均可提升性能并加快训练收敛。

Conclusion: 潜空间图生成性能滞后的关键原因之一是潜维度重要性分布不均与等权重建损失的错配；按重要性重加权是一种轻量、通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph generation has shown tremendous effectiveness in modeling complex scientific tasks ranging from molecule generation to optimized circuit design. Most recent advancements have developed models that learn to denoise noisy graphs using either discrete diffusion or flow matching. However, discrete graph diffusion scales quadratically with the number of nodes, making it difficult to use such models on larger graphs. On the other hand, latent graph generation instead seeks to denoise noisy graph latents, which scale better to more nodes due to their lower dimension. However, in practice, latent graph generation models lag behind their discrete counterparts in performance. We study this problem, finding that it is caused by the unequal distribution of that different latent dimensions have on graph reconstruction. the impact. We observe that the vast majority of impact is contained in only a smaller number of latent dimensions. Since equal emphasis is placed on reconstructing the entire latent in the loss function, existing models are optimized to reconstruct information irrelevant to the downstream task. To tackle this problem, we propose Latent Importance Reweighting ({\bf LIR)} which weights each latent dimensions via it's importance to final graph reconstruction. Importance is calculated as the relative influence of a single latent unit on graph reconstruction, which is represented via the Jacobian of the decoder relative to the latent. Our method is model-agnostic and requires no additional model parameters or hyperparameters. Experiments show that LIR can boost performance and lead to faster training convergence when applied to two different autoencoders on various benchmark datasets. Code: https://github.com/biraaj/GraphGenLatentReweight

</details>


### [147] [TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08789)
*Mathilde Papillon,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopoSHAP：一个高效通用的拓扑深度学习解释框架，包含TopoSHAP-cell（为拓扑数据单元分配Shapley值）与TopoSHAP-neighbor（解释邻域贡献并指导架构搜索）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习将图学习扩展到高阶结构（如分子环、社交网络群体），但难以解释哪些拓扑结构驱动预测，以及哪些架构组件对性能最关键。

Method: TopoSHAP-cell基于Shapley值为拓扑数据单元赋值并提取紧凑解释；TopoSHAP-neighbor量化邻域对模型性能的贡献，并将其转化为后向消除搜索，在训练早期识别更小且具竞争力的架构。

Result: 在图解释基准GraphXAI上，解释效果达到或超过基于图的对应方法，同时速度最高提升112倍；揭示了更优的拓扑特征设计，使解释恢复率与预测精度均有可测量提升；架构搜索比传统TDL训练扫描最高便宜11.3倍。

Conclusion: TopoSHAP为跨拓扑域与模型架构的数据级与架构级解释提供了统一、高效且通用的方案，兼具性能与计算效率优势。

Abstract: Topological deep learning (TDL) extends learning on graphs, such as molecules or social networks, to higher-order structures, such as rings in a molecule or groups in a social network. Explaining which of these structures drive a model's predictions, and which architectural components matter most for its performance, remains challenging. We introduce TopoSHAP, an efficient and general explanation framework that addresses both questions across topological domains and model architectures. TopoSHAP-cell assigns Shapley values to topological data cells and extracts compact explanations. These explanations match or outperform graph-based counterparts on GraphXAI benchmark data while running up to 112$\times$ faster. They also reveal advantageous topological feature design, leading to measured improvements in explanation recovery and predictive accuracy. On the architecture side, TopoSHAP-neighbor explains how neighborhoods contribute to model performance, and turns these evaluations into a backward-elimination search that identifies smaller, competitive architectures early in training. This is up to $11.3\times$ cheaper than traditional TDL training sweeps.

</details>


### [148] [MotionBench-XAI: Benchmarking What Shapes Shapley Explanations in Spatiotemporal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08797)
*Mathilde Papillon,Louisa Cornelis,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MotionBench-XAI 基准，系统评估时空数据下 Shapley 归因解释中"玩家定义、缺失填补、博弈评估"三类设计选择对解释质量的影响，并揭示分组方式比填补器更重要、代理指标在真实数据上相互矛盾等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空数据（如从人体运动预测帕金森严重程度）的预测不仅要求准确，还要求可解释（空间上的关节、时间上的窗口或二者结合）。Shapley 值的计算依赖玩家定义、缺失玩家填补方式、博弈评估方式等选择，但这些选择如何影响时空数据的解释质量尚不清楚，且真实数据缺乏可对照的真实归因。

Method: 构建 MotionBench-XAI 基准，包含：(1) 五个具有精确真实 Shapley 值的合成时空数据集；(2) 三个真实数据集（临床步态、心电图、音频）；(3) 覆盖三种玩家集合的十种归因方法；(4) 三种训练好的模型架构。

Result: 将特征按时间、空间或两者分组为玩家，比选择何种填补器影响更大；生成式填补器只有在条件模型准确时才能改善解释；在真实数据上，标准代理指标选出的最佳方法相互矛盾。

Conclusion: 作者将发现提炼为实践性建议，并开源了完整流程管线，为时空模型的可解释性评估提供可复现基准与指导。

Abstract: Predicting outcomes from spatiotemporal data, such as assessing Parkinson's severity from human motion, often requires explainability in addition to accuracy. Practitioners need to know what drove the prediction, whether it be spatial (a body joint), temporal (a specific window), or both. Shapley values are attributions computed by treating input features as players in a cooperative game and measuring how the model output changes as coalitions of players are removed. Computing them involves choosing what counts as players, how absent players are imputed, and how the game is evaluated. How these choices affect explanation quality is poorly understood for spatiotemporal data, and real data carries no ground-truth attributions to check against. We present MotionBench-XAI, a benchmark built on i) five synthetic spatiotemporal datasets with exact ground-truth Shapley values, ii) three real datasets across clinical gait, electrocardiogram, and audio, iii) ten attribution methods across three player sets, and iv) three trained architectures. We show that grouping features into players, across time, space, or both, matters more than the choice of imputer. Generative imputers improve explanations only when their conditional models are accurate. On real data, the standard proxy metrics select contradictory best methods. We distill these findings into practical insights and provide an open-source pipeline.

</details>


### [149] [On the Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models: A Few Gradient Signs Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08800)
*Nairouz Mrabah,Ismail Ben Ayed,Mahdi S. Hosseini,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向视觉-语言模型的少样本适配，提出 SignSparseSGD 与 DynSignSparseSGD 两种无状态优化器：仅用梯度幅值选择更新位置，用固定幅值的符号决定更新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本适配中监督信号有限，梯度估计噪声大；作者发现梯度幅值严重失准（miscalibrated），而幅值最大的少量分量其符号仍具信息量，因此不应直接依赖幅值大小进行更新。

Method: 两种无状态（state-free）优化器：静态版在首个 minibatch 后固定更新支撑集（support），动态版在每一步刷新支撑集；均不维护一阶/二阶动量状态，不引入任务特定模块，并对数据集、骨干网络与 shot 级别共用同一配置。

Result: 在 11 个数据集、3 个 CLIP 骨干、5 个 shot 设置下，平均性能与参数高效及基于优化的基线相当或更优，且对长训练计划的敏感性显著更低。

Conclusion: 稀疏符号更新可有效缓解少样本梯度幅值失准问题，在无需额外状态与调参的情况下实现稳健且具竞争力的适配性能。

Abstract: The limited supervision available for the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models makes gradient estimation particularly noisy. We find that the gradient magnitudes are severely miscalibrated, while the signs of a small top-magnitude subset remain informative. This motivates SignSparseSGD and DynSignSparseSGD, two state-free optimizers that use gradient magnitudes only to select where to update and fixed-magnitude signs to decide how to update. The static variant fixes the support after the first minibatch. The dynamic variant refreshes it at every step. Across 11 datasets, three CLIP backbones, and five shot levels, the proposed methods are competitive with or outperform parameter-efficient and optimization-based baselines on average, while exhibiting markedly lower sensitivity to long training schedules. They introduce no task-specific modules, maintain no first- or second-moment optimizer states, and use one shared configuration across datasets, backbones, and shot levels. Our code is included in the supplementary materials and will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [150] [What Matters for LLM Persona Simulation: Signal Form in Cold-Start E-Commerce Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08840)
*Jiwoo Choi,In Ik Lee,Woo Sung Seo,Minho Lee,Yun Young Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM人格模拟在广告冷启动效果预估中的关键设计选择，基于Google Ads电商广告五个demographic分组的真实数据，发现"仅用demographic锚点、采用相对定性信号、优化提示措辞"三种信号形式显著提升模拟质量，MAE 24.9、MAPE 26.1%，较最佳非LLM基线MAE降低51.3%，较使用显式CTR锚点的LLM配置降低77.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告上线前缺乏历史日志，冷启动场景下无法用传统日志法预估广告效果；LLM可通过人格条件化模拟用户响应，但究竟哪些设计选择真正驱动模拟质量尚不清楚，因此需要系统性识别关键信号形式。

Method: 使用真实Google Ads电商广告活动、覆盖五个demographic分组的实验数据；围绕三类信号形式维度做系统性消融：(i) 纯demographic锚点 vs. 含噪声的丰富属性；(ii) 相对定性"横幅盲视"信号 vs. 定量锚点；(iii) 提示措辞；并与直接先验、规则基线及多种LLM配置对比。

Result: 所提方法在该活动上MAE 24.9、MAPE 26.1%；MAE较最佳非LLM基线低51.3%，较使用显式CTR锚点的LLM配置低77.3%；最小化demographic描述的平均误差低于更丰富的人格配置，相对定性线索显著优于所测试的定量锚点。

Conclusion: LLM人格模拟的有效性主要取决于信号形式而非人格丰富度：简洁demographic锚点、相对定性线索与措辞优化是关键，定量CTR锚点反而有害；为冷启动广告效果预估提供了可复现的设计准则。

Abstract: Estimating advertising performance in cold-start settings is challenging because historical logs are unavailable before launch. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by simulating persona-conditioned user responses, but it remains unclear which design choices actually drive LLM-based persona simulation. Using real Google Ads e-commerce campaign data across five demographic segments, we identify three signal-form dimensions that affect simulation quality: (i) a demographic anchor without noisy rich attributes, (ii) a relative qualitative banner-blindness signal rather than quantitative anchors, and (iii) prompt phrasing. On this campaign, our method attains an MAE of 24.9 and a MAPE of 26.1%, a 51.3% lower MAE than the best tested non-LLM baseline and 77.3% lower than an LLM configuration using explicit CTR anchors. Systematic ablations show that minimal demographic descriptions achieve lower mean error than richer persona configurations, while relative qualitative cues substantially outperform the tested quantitative anchors. We additionally compare these configurations with direct-prior and rule-based baselines and with alternative LLM configurations.

</details>


### [151] [Reconstructing LLM Lineage Trees via Fine-Tuning Path Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08866)
*Sota Ogawa,Shojiro Yamabe,Daiki Nishiyama,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化 gray-box 下重建含内部节点的有向模型树问题，提出 TRACE-Edge：用非对称的逐任务对数似然偏差构造有向边权，并精确求解最大生成有向树，无需超参数与树结构约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调衍生的 LLM 大量发布，但其谱系依赖自述且不可验证；错误归属使验证者无法审计外包微调、确认许可合规、追踪父模型传给子模型的漏洞。三者都需推导方向而非仅存在性。现有仅用输出的方法只能做成对判定或无向谱系估计，恢复有向树需白盒权重访问。

Method: 在仅观测 teacher-forcing 对数似然的 gray-box 设定下，利用"微调提升训练数据似然且子模型继承父模型增幅"的性质，定义任意两模型间非对称的逐任务对数似然偏差作为微调历史痕迹，由此为每个有序模型对导出边权；TRACE-Edge 在这些边权下精确求解最大生成有向树，无超参数、无树结构约束。

Result: 在 5 种配置、共 25 棵由 3 个基座与 7 个任务构建的模型树上评估：用微调样本作探针时 parent-match 准确率达 0.74；即使使用与训练数据同分布的留出探针，平均仍优于输出相似度基线。

Conclusion: 非对称对数似然偏差可作为可验证的有向推导信号，使 gray-box 条件下无超参数地重建有向模型谱系树成为可能，并优于仅基于输出相似度的基线。

Abstract: Fine-tuned (FT) derivatives of large language models (LLMs) are now released in large numbers, yet their provenance relies on self-reports and cannot be verified. Misattributed lineage leaves a verifier unable to audit outsourced fine-tuning, confirm license compliance of derived models, or trace vulnerabilities that a parent passes to its children. All three require the direction of derivation, not merely its presence. Existing output-only methods are limited to pairwise derivation tests or undirected lineage estimation, while recovering a directed tree requires white-box access to model weights. We formalize the problem of reconstructing a directed model tree, including internal nodes, under gray-box access, where only teacher-forced log-likelihoods are observed and weights are never inspected. Fine-tuning raises the log-likelihood of the training data, and a child inherits this increase from its parent. Building on this property, we define an asymmetric per-task log-likelihood deviation between any two models as a trace of fine-tuning history, and derive from it an edge weight for each ordered pair of models. We propose TRACE-Edge, which exactly solves for the maximum spanning directed tree under these edge weights, with neither hyperparameters nor tree-structure constraints. We evaluate it on five configurations totaling 25 model trees built from three base models and seven tasks. TRACE-Edge achieves a parent-match accuracy of 0.74 when probed with the fine-tuning examples, and on average outperforms an output-similarity baseline even with held-out probes drawn from the same distribution as the training data.

</details>


### [152] [Learning to Restart: Differentiable Restart Policies for Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08883)
*Yiman Fong,Zhonglin Xie,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过连续时间极限构造可微的重启策略框架，为重启事件引入代理梯度，实现加速一阶方法重启策略的端到端学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重启机制对加速一阶方法的实际性能至关重要，但现有重启规则几乎全为人工设计。学习重启策略的障碍在于：与常规L2O不同，离散重启事件使轨迹对算法参数不可微，从而阻断基于梯度的策略优化。

Method: 借助重启策略的连续时间极限构建可微框架；为重启事件与优化轨迹引入高效可计算的代理梯度，并证明其收敛到对应的连续时间量；在L2O设定中进行训练与验证。

Result: 代理梯度在L2O中有效；学到的重启策略在二次规划与线性规划上优于标准手工自适应重启准则；并能从合成二次问题零样本迁移到真实数据集上的逻辑回归。

Conclusion: 连续时间视角可有效化解离散重启导致的不可微问题，使重启策略可学习、优于人工设计规则并具备跨问题零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Adaptive restart mechanisms, which reset the momentum or other auxiliary state of an iterative optimizer, play a central role in the empirical performance of accelerated first-order methods, yet existing restart rules remain almost entirely hand-designed. The obstruction to learning restart policies is that, unlike the typical Learning to Optimize (L2O) setting, the discrete restart events make the resulting trajectory non-differentiable in the algorithm parameters and thus block gradient-based optimization of the restart policy. We resolve this obstruction by developing a differentiable framework for restart policies through their continuous-time limit. We introduce surrogate gradients for the restart events and optimization trajectories that are efficient to compute and provably converge to their continuous-time counterparts. We validate the effectiveness of our surrogate gradients in L2O settings, training restart policies that improve upon standard handcrafted adaptive restart criteria on quadratic and linear programs, and that transfer zero-shot from synthetic quadratics to logistic regression on real datasets.

</details>


### [153] [SSF-Net: A Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network for Accurate PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08912)
*Siqi Zhao,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSF-Net，通过双分支Spatial-Spectral Fusion块（多方向Mamba空间分支+块对角MLP傅里叶谱分支）嵌入多分辨率卷积编解码器，以近线性复杂度同时捕捉PDE的局部与全局动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习PDE求解方法相比FEM效率更高，但PDE动力学同时包含局部（扰动渐进传播/衰减）与全局（整体耦合）两类特性，用低复杂度模型同时刻画二者仍是关键挑战。

Method: 核心为双分支SSF块：空间分支采用多方向Mamba，缓解其固有的因果、单向扫描偏置，以线性复杂度捕获局部交互并降低方向偏置；谱分支采用Fourier变换配合块对角MLP，以更少参数与更低复杂度捕获全局谱特征。SSF块嵌入多分辨率卷积编解码器以提取多尺度特征。

Result: 在覆盖多类任务类型的综合PDE基准上评估，并考察长时程稳定性；相比注意力类与卷积类基线，SSF-Net以更少参数取得更低预测误差，同时在空间token数量上保持近线性渐近复杂度。

Conclusion: 空间—谱双分支融合是兼顾局部与全局PDE动力学的有效低复杂度范式，在精度、参数效率与可扩展性上优于主流基线。

Abstract: Machine learning methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) significantly improve computational efficiency over traditional numerical solvers such as finite element method (FEM). However, PDE dynamics inherently span both local and global dynamics: disturbances propagate and decay gradually through the domain, while global coupling effects can influence the whole system simultaneously. Capturing both types of dynamics with a low complexity model remains a key challenge. To address this, we propose the Spatial-Spectral Fusion Network (SSF-Net), built around a dual-branch Spatial-Spectral Fusion (SSF) block comprising a spatial branch and a spectral branch. The spatial branch employs a multi-directional Mamba, which mitigates its inherent causal, single-direction scanning bias and capture local interactions with reduced directional bias at linear complexity. The spectral branch leverages a Fourier transform with a block-diagonal MLP to efficiently capture global spectral features with reduced parameter count and complexity. The SSF block is embedded in a multiresolution convolutional encoder-decoder, which extracts multi-scale features. We evaluate SSF-Net on a comprehensive PDE benchmark spanning multiple task types and assess its long-horizon stability. Compared with attention-based and convolution-based baselines, SSF-Net achieves lower prediction error with fewer parameters while retaining near-linear asymptotic complexity in the number of spatial tokens.

</details>


### [154] [Composable Causality: A Toolkit for Systematic Time-Series Causal Discovery and Treatment-Effect Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08918)
*Muhammad Hasan Ferdous,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DTCD 是一个可组合的多变量时间序列因果发现数据生成库，各组件（拓扑、机制、噪声、观测掩码、干预策略）经配置独立替换，并支持生成配对的“反事实孪生”以精确恢复个体处理效应，同时提供 8 种因果发现算法与 4 种处理效应估计器的参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实多变量时间序列缺少经核验的因果真值，因此因果发现与处理效应估计研究依赖合成数据。现有工具存在两类不足：一是发布后不可修改的固定数据集；二是每个脚本固定一种“机制—混杂—缺失”组合的脚本式生成器。二者都无法支持“固定其他属性、只改变单一属性（如稀疏度、噪声分布、缺失机制）”的受控实验。反事实评估同样存在缺口：现有模拟器多为按论文定制、机制硬编码，或仅产生观测数据。

Method: 提出 DTCD 数据生成库，产出带真值同期与滞后邻接矩阵的多变量时间序列。将拓扑、机制、噪声、观测掩码与干预策略设计为相互独立的组件，仅通过配置即可组合。反事实孪生的生成方式为：在事实（factual）rollout 中缓存随机实现，在干预（interventional）rollout 中重新注入该随机实现，从而配对生成孪生样本。基于此构建参考基准：81 种配置下评测 8 种因果发现算法，18 种配置下评测 4 种处理效应估计器。

Result: 配对反事实孪生可在数值精度范围内恢复个体处理效应。基准实验揭示了聚合指标所掩盖的失效模式，包括：标准因果发现方法无法处理缺失值，且无论缺失机制是否为信息性（informative）均如此；以及边际方差捷径（varsortability），该文对其进行了度量并通过标准化予以消除。

Conclusion: DTCD 通过组件化解耦与配置驱动组合，填补了“单因素受控实验”与“反事实配对评估”两类空白，为时间序列因果发现与处理效应估计提供了可复现、可精细操控的基准平台；同时其基准结果说明需以细粒度诊断替代聚合指标来评估算法。

Abstract: Real-world multivariate time series rarely come with verified ground-truth causal structure, so research on time-series causal discovery and treatment-effect estimation depends on synthetic data. Existing tools are either fixed datasets that cannot be modified after release or script-based generators in which each script fixes one combination of mechanism, confounding, and missingness. Neither supports controlled experiments that vary a single property, such as sparsity, noise distribution, or missingness mechanism, while holding the others fixed. Counterfactual evaluation has the same gap, since existing simulators are built per paper with hardcoded mechanisms or produce observational data only. We introduce DTCD (Datasets and Toolkit for Causal Discovery), a data generation library that produces multivariate time series with ground-truth contemporaneous and lagged adjacency matrices. Topology, mechanism, noise, observational mask, and intervention policy are independent components that compose through configuration alone. Paired counterfactual twins, produced by caching the stochastic realization during the factual rollout and re-injecting it during the interventional rollout, recover individual treatment effects to numerical precision. A reference benchmark of eight discovery algorithms on eighty-one configurations and four treatment-effect estimators on eighteen configurations exposes failure modes that aggregate metrics hide. These include the inability of standard discovery methods to handle missing values, regardless of whether the missingness mechanism is informative, and a marginal-variance shortcut (varsortability) that we measure and remove by standardization. Code and data: https://github.com/hferdous/Composable_Causality

</details>


### [155] [ResQAdam: Residual-Preserving Adaptive Optimization for Memory-Efficient Quantization Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08924)
*Liqun Ma,Shengkun Tang,Liangyu Wang,Lin Zhao,Wei Luo,Xinjun Niu,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao,Kun Zhang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ResQAdam，一种用于低比特 LLM 量化感知训练的低秩高效优化器：仅保存单份 BF16 动量，在主奇异子空间 (r×r) 上运行 Adam，并用残差符号的归一化更新补偿被投影丢弃的分量，在 2-bit 设置下相较 AdamW 降低 71%–74% 优化器状态内存且吞吐损失 <2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAT 是获得高精度低比特 LLM 最可靠的手段，但 AdamW 需为每个权重矩阵维护两份稠密 FP32 状态，内存开销巨大。现有低秩内存高效优化器（GaLore、LDAdam）直接迁移到 QAT 会显著退化：QAT 动量中有相当一部分位于 top-r 奇异子空间之外，而基于投影的方法每步都将该部分丢弃。

Method: 1) 经验分析：量化 QAT 动量在低秩子空间外的残差占比大，且该残差仅保留符号时仍具信息量；2) ResQAdam：仅维护单个 BF16 动量，Adam 更新只作用于主奇异子空间的 r×r 坐标，另对残差计算归一化 sign 更新并叠加；3) 实验覆盖两种从头训练的 QAT 方法与三种 QAT 微调流程，权重可量化至 2 bit。

Result: 相对 AdamW，优化器状态内存减少 71%–74%；在多数设置下精度接近 AdamW；训练吞吐下降不足 2%。

Conclusion: 低秩优化器在 QAT 中的失效主要源于残差动量被逐步丢弃而非低秩假设本身失效；以“低秩 Adam + 残差符号更新”的组合可在极低内存与可忽略吞吐代价下逼近 AdamW 的 QAT 效果。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) is the most reliable way to obtain accurate low-bit large language models (LLMs), but training with AdamW keeps two dense FP32 states for every weight matrix. We find that low-rank memory-efficient optimizers such as GaLore and LDAdam degrade substantially in QAT. A considerable part of the QAT momentum lies outside its top-r singular subspace, and projection-based methods discard this part at every step. We further observe that this residual remains useful when only its sign is kept. Based on these observations, we propose ResQAdam. It keeps a single BF16 momentum, applies Adam only to the r x r coordinates of the principal subspace, and adds a normalized sign update computed from the residual. We evaluate ResQAdam with two from-scratch QAT methods and three QAT fine-tuning pipelines, with weights quantized down to 2 bits. It reduces optimizer-state memory by 71% to 74% relative to AdamW, stays close to AdamW in most settings, and lowers training throughput by less than 2%.

</details>


### [156] [Ordered Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08936)
*Dongkyu Derek Cho,Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有序策略优化（OPO），用自适应、样本级的裁剪半径替代PPO的固定裁剪范围，通过对小批量重要性比率排序并依据预设尾部剖面为极端比率分配半径，在MuJoCo与Atari上取得优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO对所有权重比率使用统一固定裁剪范围，虽简单有效，但无法区分统计上有害的极端比率与幅度较大但仍可容忍的变化，缺乏对比率分布可靠性的自适应能力。

Method: 案例研究表明可靠更新需控制极端比率而非仅检验单个比率是否落在固定区间内。据此提出OPO：每个小批量内对比率排序，选出对更新影响最大的极端比率，依据预设尾部剖面（为所选极端比率指定目标值）推导自适应样本级裁剪半径，目标函数保持一阶形式，仅需小批量排序与修改后的策略损失。

Result: 在MuJoCo与Atari基准上，该简单改动相对已有基线表现出较强性能。

Conclusion: 裁剪半径应随重要性比率分布的可靠性自适应调整；以尾部剖面约束极端比率的样本级裁剪是一种低开销且有效的PPO改进方案。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) stabilizes policy updates by clipping the importance ratio between the new policy and the data-collecting policy. The standard rule uses one fixed clipping range for all samples. While this choice offers simplicity and effectiveness, it fails to distinguish statistically harmful extreme ratio values from large, yet tolerable changes. We study how to adapt the clipping radius to the reliability of the induced importance ratio distribution. Our case study demonstrates that reliable updates require controlling these extreme ratios, rather than merely verifying that individual ratios fall within a fixed interval. Motivated by this observation, we propose Ordered Policy Optimization (\textsc{OPO}). In each minibatch, \textsc{OPO} sorts the importance ratios, selects the extreme ratios that potentially have the largest effect on the update, and assigns them adaptive sample-wise radii instead of using the fixed clipping radius. These radii are derived from a prescribed tail profile, which specifies desired target values for the selected extreme ratios. The resulting objective remains first-order and requires only minibatch sorting and a modified policy loss. Experiments conducted on MuJoCo and Atari benchmarks demonstrate that this simple modification achieves strong performance relative to established baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Surrogate Quality Score: A Novel Composite Performance Metric for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08937)
*Maaz Ahmad,Iftekhar A. Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复合性能指标SQS（Surrogate Quality Score），通过归一化预测精度与模型复杂度的加权乘积实现兼顾两者的模型选择，用户参数可显式调节精度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程决策依赖高保真机器学习模型，模型选择需系统化方法；仅以未训练点预测精度为准则不足，需同时考虑复杂度、可解释性、可扩展性、物理一致性等属性。本文聚焦精度与复杂度两个属性的联合评价。

Method: 构造SQS：先对预测精度与模型复杂度分别归一化，再以加权乘积形式启发式融合为统一评分；引入用户指定参数显式控制精度-复杂度权衡，将人为判断与偏好纳入选择过程；文中对SQS公式化进行论证与说明。

Result: 通过示例演示SQS的实用性，表明其能同时基于精度与复杂度给出系统性、客观的模型排序与选择结果。

Conclusion: SQS为兼顾预测精度与模型复杂度的模型选择提供统一、可调的量化准则，但仅覆盖两属性，其余属性未纳入，且乘积式融合为启发式构造。

Abstract: The growing reliance on high-fidelity, state-of-the-art machine learning based models for engineering decision-making necessitates robust and systematic approaches for model selection. While high predictive accuracy at untrained points is a necessary trait in the final model, other attributes such as model complexity, forecastability, explainability, scalability, and physics-awareness may be equally important or relevant criteria depending on application context and user requirements. In this work, we propose a novel composite performance metric, Surrogate Quality Score (SQS) for model selection based on predictive accuracy and model complexity simultaneously. SQS heuristically integrates these two attributes through a weighted product of normalized measures, yielding a unified score to reflect overall model quality. A user-specified parameter in SQS enables explicit control over the accuracy-complexity trade-off, thereby incorporating human judgement and preference in the model selection process. We extensively discuss and justify the formulation of SQS and demonstrate its utility through an illustrative example. By combining predictive accuracy and model complexity, SQS offers a systematic and objective approach for model selection based on both attributes.

</details>


### [158] [Accuracy Is Not Value: Choosing Weak Supervisors by What the Student Inherits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08959)
*Romano Li,Xunfan Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出弱监督者的价值应以其误差经过学生学习算子后的形式来判定，而非原始误差；该"学生相对价值"在无标注数据下不可识别，但缺失信息仅为目标的一个有限维投影，结合少量干净标签即可在几何条件下以匹配的最坏情形下界弥补，并推广到岭回归学生与有限无标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上评价弱监督者（teacher/supervisor）时直接看其自身误差大小，但在"用弱监督者生成数据训练学生模型"的场景中，真正重要的是学生从中学到了什么。因此需要一个以学生为中心的价值度量，并厘清该度量能否从无标注数据中估计。

Method: 在平方损失下，对所有监督者共享的任意线性学习器，将监督者误差分解为经过学生学习算子的"学生相对误差"，分析其内积与原始误差内积的兼容性；刻画学生相对价值在无标注数据下的不可识别性，给出所需的最小信息（目标的一个有限维投影）；引入已知的教师训练规则或学生相对形式的标签模型条件，并结合干净标签与高斯设计分析加权与叠加（stacking）表现。

Result: 原始误差内积可与任何可行的学生风险值相容，按原始误差加权的最差情况在相对超额风险上可比最优加权任意差；学生相对价值不可从无标注数据识别，但缺口很小：一个有限维投影决定弱聚合是否有益，另一个决定监督者如何加权；干净标签可弥补剩余缺口，代价由未解析方向的几何而非学生表示维度决定，并给出匹配的最坏情形下界；高斯设计下量化了精确教师结构优于普通堆叠、以及弱监督者加干净标签优于仅用干净标签的条件。

Conclusion: 弱监督者的评价必须采用学生相对的视角，原始误差不足以指导加权；学生相对价值虽在无标注下不可识别，但借助极少量目标信息与干净标签即可恢复有效保证，且这些结论可扩展到拟合的岭回归学生与有限无标注数据场景。

Abstract: Weak supervisors are usually judged by their own errors, but what matters is what a student learns from them. For squared loss and any linear learner shared by all supervisors, we show that a supervisor's value is determined by its errors after they pass through the student's learning operator. Consequently, the inner products of the supervisors' raw errors can be compatible with every feasible value of the student's risk, and weighting supervisors by them can be arbitrarily worse than the best weighting in relative excess risk. This student-relative value is not identifiable from unlabeled data, but what is missing is small: one finite-dimensional projection of the target decides whether training on a weak aggregate helps, another decides how to weight the supervisors. Known teacher training rules, or a label-model condition stated in student-relative form, shrink it further. Clean labels close the remaining gap at a cost set by the geometry of the unresolved directions rather than by the dimension of the student's representation, with matching worst-case lower bounds. In Gaussian designs we also quantify when exact teacher structure beats ordinary stacking and when weak supervisors, combined with clean labels, beat every procedure that uses the clean labels alone. The guarantees extend to fitted ridge students and finite unlabeled data.

</details>


### [159] [Energy-Based Reasoners: Your Recurrent Reasoner Should Be an Energy-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08977)
*William Xingxu Chen,Alexi Gladstone,Zeyu Michael Li,Heng Ji,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Energy-Based Reasoners (EBRs)，在潜在推理状态上学习能量函数，将循环更新表述为该能量的梯度下降，并引入探索式训练，实现"何时停止循环"的显式判据与更优的多解覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 looped/recurrent 模型虽能通过反复迭代同一块扩展有效深度、在潜空间做多步推理，但缺乏显式的"进度"度量，导致难以判断何时停止迭代、也难以明确如何改进当前潜状态。

Method: 1) 学习一个对潜推理状态打分的能量函数 E(z)，把循环块的更新解释为在能量上的梯度下降，梯度既用于状态精修，也作为停止信号；2) 引入 exploration-based training，鼓励发现新的解模式（mode），从而在更广的状态集合上塑造能量景观（energy landscape），缓解仅拟合已访问状态的局限。

Result: 在四个算法推理基准上匹配或超越先前的 recurrent reasoning 模型；Sudoku-Extreme 上达 99.9% 准确率，推理算力比次优模型少 345×，训练算力相当；在所有多解基准上取得不同有效解覆盖的 SOTA，同时保持近 100% 的单样本准确率。

Conclusion: 能量模型为强推理性能提供了有前景的方向：以能量作为进度度量统一了精修与停止判据，并结合探索式训练提升多解覆盖与效率。

Abstract: Looping is a powerful inductive bias for reasoning. By repeatedly iterating a recurrent block, looped models can scale their effective depth and enable multi-step reasoning in latent space. However, existing looped models are not inherently guided by an explicit measure of progress, making it difficult to determine when to stop looping and how to improve upon the current state. To enable this, we introduce Energy-Based Reasoners (EBRs), which learn an energy function over latent reasoning states and formulate looped updates as gradient descent on this energy. The energy scores latent states, while its gradient guides refinement and provides a signal for stopping. To train EBRs effectively, we further introduce exploration-based training to discover additional modes and to better shape the energy landscape over a broader set of states. Across four algorithmic reasoning benchmarks, EBRs match or outperform prior recurrent reasoning models, achieving 99.9% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme while using 345$\times$ less inference compute than the next-best model and maintaining comparable training compute. On all multi-solution benchmarks, EBRs achieve state-of-the-art coverage of distinct valid solutions while maintaining near-100% single-sample accuracy. These results suggest that energy-based models are a promising approach for strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [160] [Action-Centered Causal Bandits for Budget-Constrained Promotion Targeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08990)
*Yifan Lin,Kaixu Yang,Di Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DR-CATE-CBwK：以"动作中心化"方式直接学习处理对照（treatment contrast）的预算约束上下文老虎机算法，在非线性基线误设下仍保持估计有效性，并在CATE线性、i.i.d.设定下得到 Õ(Kd√T) 遗憾界，半合成实验较最强基线降低约20%遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台需在全局限额下为每个用户从昂贵干预中选择一种以最大化增量效果。该问题可建模为带异质处理效应的预算约束上下文老虎机。虽然动作无关的结果基线在比较处理时会抵消，但显式建模非线性基线会抬高统计复杂度；直接对原始奖励建模还可能在共享基线误设与动作相关日志下产生对照偏差。因此需要一种直接学习处理对照、且对基线模型误设稳健的方法。

Method: 1) 动作中心化因果老虎机DR-CATE-CBwK，直接估计treatment contrast；2) 在线更新不使用逆倾向加权，在基线模型误设下仍有效；3) 离线双重稳健（DR）学习器热启动CATE估计；4) 在线动作中心化估计器用于序贯对照学习；5) 原-对偶控制器配合"可负担动作规则"，逐路径（pathwise）强制有效成本预算；6) 理论上将动作中心化估计矩的无偏性推广到任意可预测基线估计，并指出独立分臂奖励回归在动作相关日志与共享基线误设下存在渐近对照偏差；7) 实用版本采用受限Thompson采样变体。

Result: 在平稳i.i.d.、线性CATE设定下证明高概率遗憾界 Õ(Kd√T)（K为含价格动作数，d为上下文维数，T为时间跨度），并以覆盖保证动作规则下的流体线性规划（LP）为基准。理论上证明动作中心化估计矩对任意可预测基线估计保持无偏，而独立分臂奖励回归可能出现渐近对照偏差。在带真实日志特征、模拟结果的半合成就业市场基准上，受限Thompson变体在运行区间内取得所评估预算可行方法中最低遗憾，较最强奖励型基线降低约20%，优于原始奖励上下文老虎机、BwK基线与静态离线策略，且始终不超有效成本预算。

Conclusion: 通过动作中心化直接学习处理对照，可在不做逆倾向加权的情况下获得对基线误设稳健的估计，并在预算约束下取得可证明的次线性遗憾与实测更优性能；该方法兼顾离线DR热启动、在线序贯对照学习与严格预算可行性，适用于定价动作的增量效果优化场景。

Abstract: Web platforms select among costly interventions for each user to maximize incremental outcomes under a global budget. We formulate this problem as a budget-constrained contextual bandit with heterogeneous treatment effects. Although an action-independent outcome baseline cancels from treatment comparisons, modeling a nonlinear baseline can increase statistical complexity.
  We propose DR-CATE-CBwK, an action-centered causal bandit that directly learns treatment contrasts. Its online update remains valid under baseline-model misspecification without inverse-propensity weighting. It combines an offline doubly robust learner to warm-start treatment-effect estimates, an online action-centered estimator for sequential contrast learning, and a primal-dual controller with an affordable-action rule to enforce the effective-cost budget pathwise.
  For linear conditional average treatment effects (CATEs) in a stationary i.i.d. setting, we prove a high-probability (\widetilde{O}(Kd\sqrt{T})) regret bound, where K is the number of priced actions, d the context dimension, and T the horizon. We benchmark against the fluid linear program (LP) under the coverage-guaranteed action rule. We further show that the action-centered estimating moment remains unbiased under any predictable baseline estimate, whereas a specified separate-arm reward regression can exhibit asymptotic contrast bias under action-dependent logging and shared-baseline misspecification.
  On a semi-synthetic job-marketplace benchmark with real logged features and simulated outcomes, our practical Thompson-restricted variant achieves the lowest regret among evaluated budget-feasible methods in the operating regime. It reduces regret by approximately 20% versus the strongest reward-based baseline, outperforming raw-reward contextual bandits, BwK baselines, and a static offline policy, while never exceeding the effective-cost budget.

</details>


### [161] [Enforcing Symmetries on Pre-trained ML Models for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08992)
*Wenjie Zhou,Valentino F. Foit,David W. Hogg,Soledad Villar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究事后群平均（test-time augmentation）在非紧群对称性下的推广：对紧群，归一化Haar平均给出Reynolds算子并具有风险不增保证；对非紧群，提出Weyl酉技巧、数据依赖正交投影与框架平均三种替代方案，并在单位重标定群与Lorentz群上做理论与实验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后群平均无需改结构或重训练即可给预测器施加对称性，但仅在有限/紧群上有归一化Haar测度与Reynolds算子的风险保证；非紧群无法归一化Haar测度，需要寻找替代的对称化构造并评估其实际收益。

Method: 先在物理仿真模型上做紧群事后平均的数值实验；再把框架扩展到非紧群，用三种替代方案替代Haar平均：(1) Weyl酉技巧（复化空间并延拓预测器）；(2) 数据依赖的正交投影（在不变/等变函数空间上做非平凡优化）；(3) 框架平均（将点映射到轨道代表元集合）。在单位重标定群与Lorentz群上做理论推导与实验验证。

Result: 非紧情形下，Weyl技巧与框架平均相对基线无稳定提升，仅在测试数据分布外时提升数个数量级，Weyl技巧在低数据情形下也有改善；正交投影在分布内与分布外均可带来提升。直接在不变量特征上训练的模型达到或超过对无约束模型做投影的效果。

Conclusion: 事后对称化可扩展到非紧群，但并不能稳定优于直接训练对称模型；训练不变量/等变模型仍是更可靠的默认选择。

Abstract: Post-hoc group averaging (or test-time augmentation) is a simple way to impose symmetry on a trained predictor without changing its architecture or retraining it. For finite and compact groups, averaging over a normalized Haar measure on the group gives the Reynolds operator: a canonical projection onto invariant or equivariant predictors with known non-increasing risk guarantees under certain assumptions. This paper provides numerical experiments on physics emulation models that demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in this setting. We then extend this framework to non-compact group symmetries. For non-compact groups, the Haar measure cannot be normalized, so group averaging cannot be defined in the same way. We replace it with three alternatives: the Weyl unitary trick (which requires complexifying the space and extending the predictor), a data-dependent orthogonal projection (which involves a non-trivial optimization on the space of invariant or equivariant functions), and a frame averaging approach (which involves mapping each point to a set of orbit representatives). We illustrate the constructions theoretically and empirically on the unit-rescaling group and the Lorentz group. Our results show that in the non-compact case, the Weyl trick and frame averaging do not provide a consistent improvement over the baseline, except where the test data is out-of-distribution (an orders-of-magnitude improvement in this case) and, for the Weyl trick, in low-data regimes. The orthogonal projection can provide improvements in-distribution and out-of-distribution. We also observe that models trained directly on invariant features matched or beat the projection of an unconstrained one. We conclude that post-hoc symmetrization extends to non-compact groups, but it does not consistently outperform training a symmetric model.

</details>


### [162] [When2What: A Timing-Conditioned Framework for Long-Horizon Forecasting of Continuous Cardiovascular Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08993)
*Chaoqi Zhang,Shujie Yan,Zhao Zhang,Yanhua Sun,Yu Wang,Li Li,Lingzhong Meng,Xiaoyi Chen,Haixu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出When2What时序条件化框架，解耦"何时"（心搏时刻）与"何形"（搏动形态）预测，实现准周期心血管波形的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期心血管波形长期预测需同时解决心搏时间与局部形态两个问题；但以逐点MSE训练的常规序列预测器会对不确定的心搏相位取平均，导致R峰与脉搏上升沿被抹平为无特征基线。作者对术中ECG的分析揭示明显时间不对称性：由历史推断的搏动时刻可预测性数秒内即因心率变异性而衰减，而以真实搏动时刻对齐的局部形态则可稳定数分钟。这一观察为解耦时序与形态建模提供了依据。

Method: 提出When2What三阶段框架（Predict、Commit、Generate）：基于120秒历史预测60秒密集时序图（timing map），从中选取并锐化为单位高度高斯脉冲的离散心搏事件，再将已提交事件与观测历史共同作为条件输入神经波形生成器。

Result: 在术中ECG上显著优于现有时序与生理信号预测器，近期波形事件技能达0.644，并在生成波形中复现了95.62%的预测心搏，而直接长期预测器则退化为平坦基线。方法可零样本迁移至外部医院与房颤队列，并有效扩展至ABP与PPG脉搏波形；消融与合成分析支持显式时序条件化范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式时序条件化（先定"何时"、再生成"何形"）是准周期长期波形预测的有效范式，兼具准确性、可迁移性与跨信号泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon forecasting of quasi-periodic cardiovascular waveforms, such as the electrocardiogram (ECG), arterial blood pressure (ABP), and photoplethysmogram (PPG), requires predicting both when future cardiac events will occur (beat timing) and what the local structural pattern looks like around each event (beat morphology). However, standard sequence forecasters trained with pointwise mean squared error (MSE) average across uncertain future heartbeat phases, flattening sharp R peaks and pulse upstrokes into featureless baselines. Our analysis of intraoperative ECG reveals a clear temporal asymmetry: beat-time predictability from history decays within seconds due to stochastic heart-rate variability, whereas local beat morphology aligned at true beat times remains stable over several minutes. Guided by this insight, we introduce When2What, a timing-conditioned framework comprising three stages: Predict, Commit, and Generate. Given a 120-s history, When2What predicts a dense 60-s timing map, selects and sharpens discrete beat events into unit-height Gaussian impulses, and conditions a neural waveform generator on both these committed events and the observed history. Across intraoperative ECG, When2What substantially outperforms existing time-series and physiological forecasters, achieving a near-term waveform event skill of 0.644 and reproducing 95.62% of its predicted heartbeats in the generated waveform where direct long-horizon forecasters collapse into flat baselines. Furthermore, When2What transfers zero-shot to external hospital and atrial fibrillation cohorts, extends effectively to ABP and PPG pulse waveforms, and is supported by ablations and synthetic analyses that establish explicit timing conditioning as an effective paradigm for quasi-periodic long-horizon waveform forecasting.

</details>


### [163] [Calibrating your confidence requires thinking of all the ways you could be wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08997)
*Marco Miani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于Fisher信息矩阵零空间的投影退化高斯后验，兼顾贝叶斯不确定性的高质量与低复杂度，在大模型上实现SOTA的置信度校准与OOD检测，并从信息几何、微分几何与PAC-Bayes角度给出理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度网络存在置信度失准问题，传统架构缺乏量化自身不确定性的机制；扩大模型与数据只能缓解而非根除该结构性缺陷。贝叶斯方法虽提供原则性的不确定性建模框架，但计算开销常损害可扩展性或质量，这一权衡阻碍了其广泛应用。

Method: 在参数空间引入一种退化高斯分布，其协方差为与Fisher信息矩阵零空间对齐的投影矩阵；给出两种数值高效算法，分别用于后验采样与预测置信度的直接计算；理论上借助Amari信息几何分析退化结构，指出参数扰动可分解为两个正交子空间，该后验是捕获此分解的最简选择（一个子空间单位方差、另一子空间零方差），并用微分几何工具识别与修正非线性化Laplace近似中的错位问题，同时结合PAC-Bayes泛化界展开分析。

Result: 实证表明该方法在大型模型上取得State-Of-The-Art级别的置信度校准与分布外（OOD）检测性能，同时保持数值高效与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过在Fisher零空间上构造投影退化高斯后验，该论文在高质量与低复杂度之间架起桥梁，为过参数化模型的贝叶斯不确定性量化与泛化理解提供了统一的理论与算法框架。

Abstract: A primary limitation of modern deep learning is the miscalibration of model confidence; traditional architectures fundamentally lack a mechanism to quantify their own uncertainty. Larger models and larger datasets mitigate this issue, but they do not resolve the underlying structural deficiency. Bayesian methods offer a principled uncertainty-aware learning framework through stochastic parameters. Yet, despite the established benefits, the inherent computational overhead often compromises either scalability or quality. This trade-off currently prevents the widespread adoption of Bayesian methods.
  This Thesis bridges the gap between high-quality and low-complexity by introducing a novel distribution on parameter space: a degenerate Gaussian whose covariance is a projection matrix aligned with the null space of the Fisher information matrix. We demonstrate the scalability of this Bayesian posterior through two numerically efficient algorithms: one for posterior sampling and another for the direct computation of predictive confidence. Empirically, we show State-Of-The-Art confidence calibration and out-of-distribution detection for large models.
  We provide theoretical grounding for this approach. We study the degeneracies inherent in Amari's information geometry and highlight its most relevant component: the split of parameter's perturbations into two orthogonal subspaces. From this perspective, our posterior naturally emerges as the simplest choice that captures this split, assigning unitary variance to one subspace and zero variance to the other. Moreover, through the tools of differential geometry, we utilize this split to identify and rectify a critical misalignment issue of the non-linearized Laplace Approximation. Finally, with a focus on PAC-Bayes generalization bounds, we pave the way for a deeper understanding of the learning capabilities of overparametrized models.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Robust Reinforcement Learning Market Making: Lessons from Loss Function Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09012)
*Siddharth Nand,Bora Guney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究不同损失函数形式对强化学习做市系统中风险管理行为的影响，发现对库存与现金管理的惩罚能带来更稳健、适应性更强的做市策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 做市商对金融市场高效交易至关重要，近期研究开始用强化学习训练做市系统；但损失函数形式如何影响风险管理行为尚缺乏系统考察。

Method: 实证评估方法：在强化学习做市框架下对比多种损失函数设计，将纯利润最大化策略与风险规避型（含库存、现金惩罚项）策略进行对照。

Result: 加入库存与现金管理惩罚的风险规避策略相比利润最大化策略表现更稳健、适应性更强。

Conclusion: 算法交易中需在利润最大化与风险管理之间取得平衡；研究为实际场景中的策略优化提供了可操作的洞见。

Abstract: Market makers play a vital role in ensuring efficient trade within financial markets. Recent literature has delved into training market-making systems using reinforcement learning. This paper delves into the impact of various loss function formulations on risk management behavior within reinforcement-based market making. Through empirical evaluation, we contrast profit-maximizing strategies with risk-averse approaches, finding that penalties for inventory and cash management foster more resilient and adaptable market-making strategies. Our results underscore the significance of striking a balance between profit maximization and risk management in algorithmic trading, providing actionable insights for refining strategies in practical settings.

</details>


### [165] [KnowEvo: Knowledge Evolution for Protein Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09013)
*Zijie Xing,Xingyue Liu,Runze Wang,Luoming Hu,Yanming Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KnowEvo将外部化知识本身作为优化目标，把知识表示为可执行的"专长模块"，并将迭代改进组织为策略变异树，结合Thompson式前向预算分配与沙盒内代码智能体的反向局部修订，实现机制性专长的持续积累、评估与精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的科学发现方法主要通过参数优化或对提示、程序、求解轨迹的反馈式改进来提升问题求解能力，导致获得的知识要么内嵌于模型权重，要么散落在迭代求解过程中，难以被系统地检视、评估和精炼。

Method: 核心洞察是科学知识具有模块性——多个相互作用的机制共同决定设计结果，因此改进难以归因到单一组件。据此，KnowEvo将知识表示为可执行的专长模块（executable expertise blocks），把模块修订组织成策略变异树，以在组合知识空间上做结构化探索；并采用Thompson式前向分配策略自适应地在策略变体间分配评估预算，配合反向修订过程：代码智能体在隔离沙盒中把rollout证据转化为局部化的程序更新。

Result: 在蛋白质优化任务（稳定性与溶解度）上，知识演化相较初始策略模块持续提升，并在pass@k、MRR、MAP、NDCG@5等指标上优于单体程序演化及已有的LLM引导演化基线。

Conclusion: 将外部化、模块化的可执行知识作为优化对象，可在迭代实验中实现机制性专长的持久积累与精炼，是一种比参数优化或单块程序演化更有效的LLM科学发现范式；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in scientific discovery, yet systematically accumulating and refining reusable scientific knowledge remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily improve LLM-based problem solving through parameter optimization or feedback-driven refinement of prompts, programs, and solution trajectories, leaving acquired expertise either embedded in model weights or scattered across iterative problem-solving processes. As a result,knowledge is difficult to systematically inspect, evaluate, and refine. We propose KnowEvo, a framework that treats externalized knowledge itself as the optimization target. Our key insight is that scientific knowledge is inherently modular: multiple interacting mechanisms jointly determine the design outcomes. This makes knowledge evolution fundamentally challenging, as improvements cannot be attributed to individual components in isolation. To address this, KnowEvo represents knowledge as executable expertise blocks and organizes their refinements into strategy mutation trees, enabling structured exploration over a combinatorial knowledge space. The framework combines a Thompson-style forward allocation strategy that adaptively distributes evaluation budgets across strategy variants with a backward revision process, in which a code agent operates in an isolated sandbox to translate rollout evidence into localized program updates. This design enables persistent accumulation, evaluation, and refinement of mechanistic expertise across iterative experimentation. We evaluate KnowEvo on protein optimization tasks including stability and solubility. Results show that knowledge evolution consistently improves over initial strategy blocks and outperforms monolithic program evolution and prior LLM-guided evolution baselines across metrics such as pass@k, MRR, MAP,and NDCG@5. Code is available at https://github.com/recynie/KnowEvo.

</details>


### [166] [Knowledge Distillation from Neural Networks to Differentiable Logic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09024)
*Hee Yun Suh,Niraj K. Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将知识蒸馏引入可微逻辑网络（DLN），以训练好的MLP为教师、DLN为学生，在不改变结构或推理流程的前提下提升DLN精度并保留其可解释性与低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLN虽可解释、边友好且推理成本极低，但受梯度消失、可扩展性差及生态缺失的限制，在多个数据集上精度不及传统神经网络，存在性能差距需要弥补。

Method: 从训练好的MLP教师向DLN学生进行知识蒸馏，将蒸馏目标融入两阶段训练循环（神经元函数阶段与连接阶段），训练后丢弃教师。研究四种变体：联合超参搜索（同时优化蒸馏权重、温度与架构）、固定架构下对蒸馏权重与温度的网格搜索，以及仅在神经元函数阶段或仅在连接阶段使用蒸馏的两个单阶段变体。

Result: 在20个公开表格分类任务上，蒸馏在门级操作数与模型规模几乎不变的情况下提升了平衡类别准确率，收益因数据集而异，最高提升15%，部分情况下甚至超过教师模型。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏能在几乎不增加推理成本与模型规模的前提下有效缩小DLN与MLP的精度差距，且蒸馏后的模型仍保持传统DLN的可解释性、稀疏性与推理成本。

Abstract: Differentiable logic networks (DLNs) are interpretable, edge-friendly classifiers that learn both their logic operators and their connections through gradient descent, achieving accuracy competitive with multi-layer perceptrons at orders-of-magnitude lower inference cost. Their accuracy, however, is bounded by the problems of vanishing gradients, scalability, and lack of an ecosystem, leaving a gap relative to conventional neural networks on several datasets. We introduce knowledge distillation for DLNs to narrow this gap. We distill from a trained multi-layer perceptron teacher into a DLN student, integrating the distillation objective into a two-phase training loop without altering the architecture or the inference procedure. The teacher is discarded after training. Hence, the distilled model retains the interpretability, sparsity, and inference cost of a conventionally trained DLN. We investigate this approach through four variants: a joint hyperparameter search that optimizes the distillation weight and temperature alongside the architecture, a grid search over these two hyperparameters with the architecture fixed, and two single-phase variants that employ distillation during only the neuron-function or only the connection phase. On 20 public tabular classification tasks, distillation improves balanced-class accuracy at negligible cost to gate-level operation counts and model size, though the benefit varies by dataset. Performance improvements of up to 15% are observed, in some cases surpassing the teacher itself.

</details>


### [167] [Function-Space Certification for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09038)
*Ryan Hsiang,Robert Joseph George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数空间认证框架，将IBP与CROWN推广到函数空间，对神经算子在整个连续域上给出严格上下界保证，并可扩展到高阶导数以认证PDE残差与解误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子学习的是无穷维函数空间之间的PDE解算子映射，而现有神经网络验证方法面向向量到向量的映射，只能在固定网格分辨率上认证，无法对整个定义域及跨分辨率给出保证，因此需要函数空间层面的严格认证。

Method: 在函数空间中重新表述区间界传播（IBP）与CROWN，利用严格的数值积分逐层传播上下界函数；进一步将界传播推广到高阶导数，用于认证PDE残差与解误差界。

Result: 在多种神经算子架构与多个科学问题上验证，包括用于全球天气预报的5.725亿参数球面傅里叶神经算子，方法能高效产生紧致且覆盖整个定义域的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经算子提供了域级、跨分辨率的严格认证基础，是可信算子学习的重要一步。

Abstract: Certifying the robustness and physical consistency of scientific machine-learning models ensures that their predictions remain reliable beyond the test set. For neural operators, which learn PDE solution operators by mapping infinite-dimensional function spaces, certification means establishing rigorous, domain-wide guarantees for their output functions. However, standard neural network (NN) verification methods are fundamentally designed for vector-to-vector mappings, and they can only certify neural operators at fixed grid resolutions and provide no guarantees over the entire domain or across another resolution. We close this gap by introducing a function-space certification framework that verifies neural operators across the entire continuous domain. We formulate interval bound propagation (IBP) and CROWN in function space, propagating upper and lower bound functions through each layer using rigorous numerical integration. In addition, we extend bound propagation to higher-order derivatives to certify PDE residuals and solution error bounds. Experiments across several neural operator architectures and scientific problems, including the 572.5-million-parameter spherical Fourier neural operator for global weather forecasting, show that our method produces tight, domain-wide guarantees efficiently, providing a foundation for trustworthy operator learning.

</details>


### [168] [When Can You Trust Neural Critics? Auditing Mutual Information Estimates of Higher-Order Epistasis in Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09046)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Waldemar Karwowski,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文审计了ESM-2在GB1四位点景观上的序分辨上位性互信息（MI）研究，发现变分MI估计器在高维盲估计下信号严重低估，既便估计器已稳定，也不足以支撑序分辨依赖结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互信息估计被用于探测神经表征（如蛋白质语言模型）编码了什么，但变分MI估计器的方差随真实MI至少指数增长，威胁此类序分辨上位性结论的可靠性，故需系统审计与诊断。

Method: 复现并审计既有ESM-2/GB1研究：比较运行间差异与跨种子重训练波动，检验评估配对方案的影响；在原始维度上对比三类替代估计器族的跨种子稳定性；在两种其他蛋白语言模型维度上构造已知MI的高斯信道进行合成校准；并用给定真实信号方向的对照critic（降维至一维）检验信号可恢复性。

Result: 运行间差异无系统性偏差，幅度与常规跨种子重训练变异相当；评估配对方案会显著改变得分。原始维度下三类估计器族中两类跨种子稳定、一类不稳定。合成校准中三类族均实质性低估信号，尽管信号排序正确、种子稳定。对照critic在增益1.5、5120与4096维下分别恢复93.3%与98.7%的解析MI。

Conclusion: 信号在特权信息下可恢复，但盲估计失败的机制尚未被对照实验识别。作者将检查封装为可复用诊断协议，并认为即便估计器已稳定，在当前维度与样本量下仍不足以支持盲搜索下的序分辨依赖主张。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) estimation is used to probe what neural representations encode, including in protein language models, but variational MI estimators have variance that scales at least exponentially in the true MI. We audit a prior neural-MI study of order-resolved epistasis in ESM-2 on the four-site GB1 landscape. Run-to-run differences show no systematic bias and a magnitude comparable to ordinary cross-seed retraining variability, and the evaluation-pairing scheme shifts scores substantially. At the original dimensionality, two of three alternative estimator families stabilize the estimate across seeds and one does not. In a synthetic calibration at the representation dimensionalities of two other protein language models (a Gaussian channel with known MI; no model representations), all three families recover materially less signal than required, despite correct signal ordering and stable seeds. A control critic given the true signal direction, which also reduces its input to one dimension, recovers 93.3% and 98.7% of the analytic MI at gain 1.5 (at 5,120 and 4,096 dimensions), so the signal is recoverable with privileged information, but the control does not identify why blind estimation fails. We package the checks into a reusable diagnostic protocol and conclude that even a stabilized estimator is not currently sufficient for order-resolved dependence claims made under blind search at this dimensionality and sample size.

</details>


### [169] [LeanDojo-v2: A Comprehensive Library for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09048)
*Ryan Hsiang,William Adkisson,Robert Joseph George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文介绍 LeanDojo-v2 与 Lean4Code：一个端到端 Lean 形式化定理证明框架（涵盖仓库数据抽取、LLM 微调、证明搜索、AI 自动补全与 LLM 辅助证明）及其配套的数学家友好型轻量 IDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 Lean 机器学习定理证明工具链割裂、配置复杂，数学家和研究者难以在领域特定 Lean 代码库上微调模型并便捷地试验 LLM 辅助证明。

Method: 以单一软件库统一仓库数据抽取、LLM 微调、证明搜索、AI 自动补全与 LLM 辅助定理证明五个环节；并构建 Lean4Code 轻量 IDE，以简洁、面向数学家的 UI 集成上述全部功能。

Result: 提供一个端到端集成框架与配套 IDE，使用户无需复杂环境配置即可完成领域自适应微调与 LLM 辅助证明实验。

Conclusion: LeanDojo-v2 与 Lean4Code 降低了 Lean 形式化证明中应用 LLM 的门槛，为数学家与研究者提供易用的实验平台。注：摘要为系统/工具描述，未给出量化评测结果。

Abstract: Building on recent progress in machine learning for formal theorem proving in Lean, we introduce LeanDojo-v2: an end-to-end framework that unifies repository data extraction, LLM fine-tuning, proof search, AI autocompletion, and LLM-assisted theorem proving in a single software library. In addition, we provide Lean4Code, a lightweight IDE that integrates the various features of LeanDojo-v2 through a simple, mathematician-friendly UI. These tools will enable mathematicians and researchers to easily fine-tune language models on domain-specific Lean codebases and experiment with LLM-assisted theorem proving without a complex setup.

</details>


### [170] [StreamTab: Training-Free Temporal Link Prediction with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09052)
*Mingqi Yang,Renqi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 StreamTab，基于冻结的表格基础模型，通过"regime conditioning"按候选采样方式构造负样本示例，用于时序链接预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图神经网络需为每个图单独训练，且模型如何利用交互历史取决于所比较的候选对：当负样本也曾交互过时，单靠"是否曾交互"无法区分，需要其他信号。因此需要一种无需训练、能随候选分布自适应的方法。

Method: StreamTab 用候选对过去交互的统计量描述每个候选对，将近期观测交互与采样得到的负样本对作为带标签示例输入冻结的表格基础模型（仅在合成表格上预训练），由该模型给新候选打分。核心设计为 regime conditioning：按候选的采样方式采样负例，使示例体现模型必须做出的区分；新交互到来时仅更新统计量与示例，模型参数保持不变。

Result: 在13个数据集上与21个时序图基线对比：历史采样下12个数据集取得最高平均精度（AP），归纳采样下11个最高，随机采样下7个数据集排名第一或与最优相差不超过1个点。在相同特征与模型下，regime conditioning 相比随机负例示例使历史采样下的AP中位数提升12.6个点。

Conclusion: 免训练的表格基础模型配合按采样机制构造示例的 regime conditioning，可在多种负采样设定下超越专门的时序图神经网络基线，说明负例分布对齐是时序链接预测的关键因素。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction uses past interactions to predict whether two nodes will interact in the future. Temporal graph neural networks achieve strong results on this task, but they are trained separately for each graph. How a model should use interaction history also depends on the candidates it is compared with. A past interaction can distinguish a recurring link from a random pair that has never interacted, but when the negative candidates have also interacted before, other signals are needed. We propose StreamTab, a training-free framework built on a frozen tabular foundation model pretrained only on synthetic tables. StreamTab describes each candidate pair by statistics of its past interactions and shows the model recent observed interactions and sampled negative pairs as labeled examples, from which the model scores new candidates. Its central design, regime conditioning, samples the negative examples according to how the candidates are sampled, so the examples show the distinction the model must make. As new interactions arrive, StreamTab updates the statistics and refreshes the examples while the model parameters stay fixed. On thirteen datasets against 21 temporal graph baselines, StreamTab achieves the highest average precision on twelve datasets under historical sampling and eleven under inductive sampling, and ranks first or stays within one point of the best on seven datasets under random sampling. With the same features and model, regime conditioning raises average precision over examples with random negatives by a median of 12.6 points under historical sampling.

</details>


### [171] [WearableBridge: Task-Guided Latent Transport for Free-Living Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09067)
*Jingjing Zou,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WearableBridge：固定参考编码器与分类器，仅学习一个"桥"将腕部特征传输到分类器对应活动区域，以有限参考数据显著提升腕戴设备的自由生活活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕戴加速度计可大规模部署，但手臂附带运动、设备朝向和情境漂移会模糊活动信号；高质量参考（同步大腿记录或有监督实验室腕戴数据）昂贵、覆盖人少且部署时难以获得。研究问题：如何用有限参考数据提升仅有腕戴设备的参与者的识别性能。

Method: WearableBridge 保持参考编码器与分类器不变，学习任务引导的传输映射（bridge），把每个腕部特征搬运到分类器关联该活动的区域；目的地由参考特征或分类器预测指示，并用分类损失惩罚落入其他活动区域的腕部特征。实例化三种桥：Mapper（回归到参考特征）、Distillation（匹配分类器类概率）、MeanFlow（从腕部到参考特征的单步流）。

Result: 在 40 名留出 PAAWS 自由生活参与者上，三种桥在每次训练运行中均提升 macro-F1；最强的 MeanFlow 在 Thigh2Wrist 下将平均 macro-F1 从 35.1% 提升至 56.0%，在 Lab2Freeliving 下从 54.0% 提升至 63.5%；仅在 PAAWS 上拟合也在 CAPTURE-24 队列上从 42.8% 提升至 54.7%。收益在 selfBACK 上复现，跨四种参考编码器保持，并随时间上下文增加而增大。

Conclusion: 研究可仅对校准子集采集参考记录，训练仅 0.13–2.44 百万参数的桥而保持参考编码器与分类器不变，即可提升其余所有参与者的纯腕戴识别性能，为有限参考数据的可扩展部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Wrist-worn accelerometers make activity monitoring feasible at scale, but incidental arm motion, device orientation, and shifting context blur the signal that separates activities. Calibration studies supply higher-quality references such as concurrent thigh recordings or supervised laboratory wrist sessions, but they are expensive, cover few participants, and are rarely available at deployment. How can limited reference data improve recognition for participants who wear only a wrist device? We propose WearableBridge, which holds the reference encoder and classifier fixed and learns a task-guided transport map, the bridge, that carries each wrist feature into the region the classifier associates with its activity. Reference features or the classifier's predictions indicate the destination, and a classification loss penalizes wrist features that land in the region of another activity. We instantiate WearableBridge with three bridges: Mapper regresses onto reference features, Distillation matches the classifier's class probabilities, and MeanFlow learns a one-step flow from wrist toward reference features. On 40 held-out free-living PAAWS participants, all three bridges improve macro-F1 in every training run, and the strongest, MeanFlow, raises mean macro-F1 from 35.1% to 56.0% with concurrent thigh references (Thigh2Wrist), and from 54.0% to 63.5% with group-matched laboratory wrist references (Lab2Freeliving). Fitted only on PAAWS, MeanFlow also raises macro-F1 on the CAPTURE-24 cohort from 42.8% to 54.7%. The gains replicate on selfBACK, hold across four reference encoders, and grow with temporal context. A study can therefore collect reference recordings for a calibration subset and improve wrist-only recognition for every other participant, training only a bridge of 0.13 to 2.44 million parameters while the reference encoder and classifier stay unchanged.

</details>


### [172] [Large Language Model Deployment on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices: A Practitioners Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09075)
*Rahul Maliakkal,Yashasvi Makin,Plawan Rath,Rohit Jain,Ankur Sadhoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了使大语言模型（LLM）能够在移动与边缘设备上部署的最新软硬件优化技术，指出边缘部署在本地数据处理、成本、时延和个性化方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽革新了AI诸多领域，但其巨大资源需求限制了在移动与边缘设备上的部署；如何在资源受限的边缘端运行LLM成为下一关键挑战，而近期软件优化与专用硬件的进展使其变得可行。

Method: 文献综述方法：梳理并归纳面向边缘设备LLM部署的最新软硬件优化技术（如模型压缩、量化、专用加速硬件等），以部署可行性与边缘优势为分析框架。

Result: 归纳出边缘部署的可行技术路径，并总结其收益：本地数据处理、成本节约、时延降低及个性化响应。

Conclusion: 软硬件协同优化已使LLM边缘部署具备可行性；边缘部署兼具隐私、成本、时延与个性化优势，是LLM落地的重要方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed many areas of artificial intelligence (AI), but their substantial resource requirements limit their deployment on mobile and edge devices. Although LLMs have demonstrated impressive capabilities in various applications, the next challenge is deploying these models on resource-limited edge devices. Recent innovations in software optimizations and specialized hardware have made it feasible to deploy LLMs on edge devices. Edge deployment offers benefits including local data processing, cost savings, reduced latency, and personalized responses. This paper reviews the latest techniques that make the deployment of LLMs on edge devices feasible.

</details>


### [173] [BrainCoT: A Multi-Task Zero-Shot Brain Signal Foundation Model with Neurometric-Anchored Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09082)
*Yiyu Gui,Mingzhi Chen,Guibo Luo,Yuchao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BrainCoT——具备神经度量锚定思维链推理的多任务零样本脑信号基础模型，无需下游适配即可直接跨任务分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号存在多中心异质性、强被试间差异与任务设置高度多样，导致难以构建跨任务泛化模型；现有脑信号基础模型仍需目标数据特定的监督适配、缺乏多任务零样本能力，且很少提供可验证的预测依据。

Method: 将异构任务统一为指令驱动框架，预训练后单模型可直接零样本用于多任务；引入基于神经度量证据锚定的思维链以实现可验证推理；采用决策一致性理由训练（Decision-Consistent Rationale Training）使过程学习与正确预测对齐。

Result: 在无下游训练数据的零样本分类中平均ACC达67.41%、AUC达74.33%，相较用1%标注下游数据训练的最强线性探测基线分别高出3.85%（ACC）与5.97%（AUC）。

Conclusion: BrainCoT以指令统一+神经度量锚定CoT+决策一致性训练，实现了可验证的多任务零样本脑信号分析，性能超过使用少量标注数据的有监督基线。

Abstract: Brain signals exhibit substantial multi-center heterogeneity, strong inter-subject variability, and highly diverse task settings, which makes it difficult to build models that generalize beyond a single task. While recent brain signal foundation models leverage large-scale pretraining to learn generalizable representations, they often fall short in practice because they still require target-data-specific supervised adaptation, lack multi-task zero-shot capability, and rarely provide verifiable evidence to support their predictions. In this work, we present BrainCoT, a multi-task zero-shot Brain signal foundation model with neurometric-anchored Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. BrainCoT unifies heterogeneous tasks into an instruction-driven framework so that, after pretraining, a single model can be directly applied to multiple tasks in zero-shot settings without additional adaptation. It further introduces neurometric-anchored chain-of-thought grounded in neurometric evidence for verifiable reasoning, and adopts Decision-Consistent Rationale Training to keep process learning aligned with correct predictions. Across extensive evaluations, BrainCoT achieves an average ACC of 67.41% and AUC of 74.33% in zero-shot classification without downstream training data, outperforming the strongest linear-probing baseline trained with 1% labeled downstream data by 3.85% in ACC and 5.97% in AUC.

</details>


### [174] [TUGB: A Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09091)
*Luzhan Yuan,Chao Mi,Xiahao Shi,Yi Mao,Shengsheng Niu,Xin Zhu,Jie He,Zhangang Lin,Ching Law*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TUGB：将生成式序列建模与强化学习结合的两阶段不确定性感知自动出价框架，先预训练生成出价模型，再用集成式不确定性环境模型微调，实现从RTG条件生成到直接出价优化的演进，并在JD搜索广告与外卖广告大规模落地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decision Transformer类生成式方法用于自动出价时存在三重缺陷：静态离线数据探索受限、对环境动态漂移脆弱、依赖启发式RTG（return-to-go）指定，导致在未见流量上表现次优。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线：第一阶段预训练生成式出价模型（Generative Bidding Model）；第二阶段通过集成（ensemble）式不确定性感知环境模型进行微调，量化认知不确定性以惩罚高风险轨迹，并将策略从RTG条件生成逐步过渡到直接出价优化。

Result: 大量实验显示TUGB显著优于SOTA基线；在JD搜索广告全量部署带来成本提升2.32%、目标成本提升3.57%；并在后续机制升级及JD外卖广告上成功适配，验证泛化能力。

Conclusion: 生成式建模与RL的两阶段融合可有效缓解离线探索不足与RTG依赖问题，不确定性惩罚提升策略鲁棒性，工业级部署结果证明其实际效能与跨场景泛化性。

Abstract: Generative modeling, particularly Decision Transformers, shows promise in auto-bidding strategies. However, it struggles with limited exploration in static offline datasets, vulnerability to dynamic environmental shifts, and a reliance on heuristic return-to-go (RTG) specifications that may yield suboptimal performance on unseen traffic. To address these challenges, we propose the Two-stage Uncertainty-aware Generative Auto-Bidding (TUGB) framework. TUGB integrates generative sequence modeling with Reinforcement Learning (RL). It employs a two-stage pipeline where a pre-trained Generative Bidding Model is fine-tuned via an ensemble-based Uncertainty-aware Environment Model. This approach quantifies epistemic uncertainty to penalize high-risk trajectories and evolves the policy from RTG-conditioning to direct bidding optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TUGB significantly outperforms SOTA baselines. Notably, full-scale deployment on JD Search Ad achieved a 2.32\% increase in cost and 3.57\% in target cost, validating its industrial efficacy, while its successful adaptation to subsequent mechanism upgrades and JD Food Delivery Ad further validates TUGB's superior generalization capability.

</details>


### [175] [COMBIO: Automated Discovery of a Compact Composite Biomarker from Routine Laboratory Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09102)
*Tomer D. Meirman,Lior S. Rokach,Bracha Shapira,Seffi Cohen,Noa Dagan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMBIO将梯度提升模型蒸馏为"乘积幂次"形式的闭式生物标志物，在六个任务上AUC与用相同五个指标的逻辑回归相差≤0.03，表明乘法表示的价值在于紧凑性而非精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于常规检验面板的机器学习模型预测效果较好，但难以解释、难以落地操作；论文希望把复杂模型压缩成简洁、可解释、易计算的闭式生物标志物。

Method: COMBIO为自动化流水线：以梯度提升模型为参考，用Shapley加性解释值对候选检验指标排序并确定允许的指数符号；复合指标为若干检验测量值的乘积、各因子取−2到2的整数幂，指数符号表示该指标升高是增大还是减小复合值；在小整数指数空间中穷举搜索，于内部验证集选公式，再在保留测试集上评估。评测覆盖六项任务：三项NHANES结局、印度肝病患者数据集肝病分类、MIMIC-IV院内死亡与脓毒症筛查，两项院内任务另在eICU做外部验证。

Result: 六个测试集上，COMBIO的AUC与使用同样五个候选检验指标的逻辑回归相比差异不超过0.03；院内死亡复合指标在外部eICU数据库AUC为0.698；仅用源医院数据库广泛可得指标的版本，在覆盖92%住院的队列中AUC为0.786。

Conclusion: 乘法乘积表示的收益是紧凑性与可操作性，而非额外精度；临床应用前需进行本地验证与重新校准。

Abstract: Machine-learning models based on routine laboratory panels can predict outcomes but can be difficult to interpret and operationalize. We present COMBIO, an automated pipeline that distils such a model into a compact closed-form biomarker. The composite is a product of laboratory measurements raised to integer powers from minus two to two. The sign of each exponent indicates whether higher values of the corresponding measurement increase or decrease the composite. Shapley additive explanation values from the gradient-boosted reference model rank five candidate measurements and guide eligible exponent signs.
